For example, I might say to a friend or a family member “Close the door, please.” But to a stranger, I probably would say, “Would you mind closing the door?” Another difference between formal and informal language is some of the vocabulary. There are bound to be some words and phrases that belong in formal language and others that are informal. Let’s say that I really like soccer. If I am talking to my friend, I might say, “I am just crazy about soccer.” But if I were talking to my boss, I would probably say, “I really enjoy soccer.”

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

OK! When we met last week, we talked about different kinds of short stories and some well-known short story writers. I remember that many of you said you liked the American writer Earnest Hemingway, right? I hope you all followed my advice and finished reading his story “A day’s wait”, because as I said, we’re going to study it together in today’s lesson. Now since you’re supposed to have read this story, let’s have some discussion. Please look at the four questions on the blackboard. First, when and where does the story take place? Second, what does the boy read for in the story and what kind of person does he show himself to be? Third, what kind of person is the boy’s father? Four, what is the writer’s main purpose of writing the story? I’d like you to work in groups of four or five and present your answers in ten minutes. Is everything clear?

听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

M: Hello!

W: Hello, Mike! This is Jane.

M: Oh. Hello, Jane!

W: Listen, Mike! I've got a real problem.

M: What is it?

W: The car's broken down.

M: Oh, not again.

W: Yeah. I checked the oil and I checked almost everything. It's just not starting at all.

M: You have to get rid of it, you know.

W: I know, I know. But look, the thing is, I've got to pick Tom up at the airport. Can I...?

M: Use my car? Yes, of course.

W: That'll be great! It's very kind of you.

M: Come on when you like. I don't need it till tonight.

W: OK! I'll be around in about an hour. Thanks a lot!

M: It's all right. See you then!

W: See you!

听第5段材料，回答第17至20题。

We experience different forms of the Sun's energy every day. We can see its light and feel its warmth. The

Sun is the major source of evaporation of water from the oceans and lakes. Sunlight also provides the energy used by green plants to make their own food. These green plants then provide food for all organisms on the Earth.

Much of the energy that comes from the Sun never reaches the Earth's surface. It is either reflected or absorbed by the gases in the upper atmosphere. Of the energy that reaches the lower atmosphere, 30% is reflected by clouds or the Earth's surface. The remaining 70% warms the surface of the planet, causes water to evaporate, and provides energy for the water cycle and weather. Only a tiny part, approximately 0.023%, is actually used by green plants to produce food.

Many gases found in the atmosphere actually reflect heat energy escaping from the Earth's surface back to the Earth. These gases act like the glass of a greenhouse in that they allow energy from the Sun to enter but prevent energy from leaving. They are therefore called greenhouse gases.

When sunlight strikes an object, some of the energy is absorbed and some is reflected. The amount reflected depends on the surface. For example, you've probably noticed how bright snow is when sunlight falls on it. Snow reflects most of the energy from the Sun, so it contributes to the low temperatures of winter. Dark-colored surfaces, such as dark soil or forest, absorb more energy and help warm the surrounding air.

##### Section II Use of English

【参考译文】11岁的女孩Angela神经系统有些问题。她不能走路，甚至更严重的是她根本就不能动。尽管她认为自己康复的机会很大，但医生说，如果得上这种病，几乎没人能恢复正常。听到这种说法之后，小女孩并没有失去信心。从此，她躺在医院的病床上，无论医生说什么，她都坚信自己肯定能重返学校。

她被转到了特殊护理中心，而且任何可以尝试的办法都被用上了。但是她还是站不起来。她似乎是打不败的。医生们都很喜欢她，并且教她想象自己能成功。每天Angela躺在那儿，虔诚地做脑部练习。

一天，当她又想象自己腿能动的时候，奇迹似乎发生了：床开始动了。她尖叫道：“看，看我在做什么！我成功了！我能动了！我能动了！”

当然，此刻医院里的其他人都感到很害怕。更重要的是，他们是在为了寻求安全而跑。

有的人在哭，设备掉落在地上。你知道了，地震发生了。但是不要把这点告诉Angela。她还以为是自己成功了呢，就像她从来没怀疑过自己能康复一样。几年之后，她又回到了学校。你知道，对于那样一个能晃动地球的人来说，那种疾病难道不就是小问题吗？

21. D 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。本题可以从下文提到的几个线索去考虑：“the doctors said that _____, if any, could come back to no/ $ \cdots $”, “Having heard this, the little girl...”从这两句就说明她能听也能说，否则就不能和医生交流；还有“her going back to/…”，以及后几段里多次出现的“move”一词就知道她不能走动，故应选D。

22. C 【解析】本题考查语境的联系和词义辨析。本题与上一题相辅相成，因为生病所以不能走路，甚至更严重的是根本就不能动，故应选 C。

23. B 【解析】本题考查词的搭配。小女孩非常坚强、执着对自己非常有信心，所以才认为自己康复的机会很大。have/stand a good/fair chance of... 意为“大有…的希望”，所以应选 B。

24. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。小女孩认为自己会康复，但医生却不这么认为，后边说没有任何人，前边应该是说相对好的情况，所以应该是几乎没有。故应选 A。

25. D 【解析】本题考查句意理解。上文医生告诉了她一个不好的消息，通过下文我们知道她并没有因此放弃，而是靠自己的努力创造了奇迹，所以她应该是没有失去信心。satisfied，满意的；delighted，高兴的；surprised，惊讶的；discouraged，失去信心的。所以应选D。

26. A 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。insist，坚持；sigh，叹气；fear，恐惧；promise，承诺。根据句意应该是她坚信自己能复原。所以应选 A。

27. C 【解析】本题考查句意理解。true，真实的；doubtful，令人怀疑的；certain，确定的，肯定的；

## 全国英语 等级考试 全真 模拟试题第4级

112

Sun is the major source of evaporation of water from the oceans and lakes. Sunlight also provides the energy used by green plants to make their own food. These green plants then provide food for all organisms on the Earth.

Much of the energy that comes from the Sun never reaches the Earth's surface. It is either reflected or absorbed by the gases in the upper atmosphere. Of the energy that reaches the lower atmosphere, 30% is reflected by clouds or the Earth's surface. The remaining 70% warms the surface of the planet, causes water to evaporate, and provides energy for the water cycle and weather. Only a tiny part, approximately 0.023%, is actually used by green plants to produce food.

Many gases found in the atmosphere actually reflect heat energy escaping from the Earth's surface back to the Earth. These gases act like the glass of a greenhouse in that they allow energy from the Sun to enter but prevent energy from leaving. They are therefore called greenhouse gases.

When sunlight strikes an object, some of the energy is absorbed and some is reflected. The amount reflected depends on the surface. For example, you've probably noticed how bright snow is when sunlight falls on it. Snow reflects most of the energy from the Sun, so it contributes to the low temperatures of winter. Dark-colored surfaces, such as dark soil or forest, absorb more energy and help warm the surrounding air.

##### Section II Use of English

【参考译文】11岁的女孩Angela神经系统有些问题。她不能走路，甚至更严重的是她根本就不能动。尽管她认为自己康复的机会很大，但医生说，如果得上这种病，几乎没人能恢复正常。听到这种说法之后，小女孩并没有失去信心。从此，她躺在医院的病床上，无论医生说什么，她都坚信自己肯定能重返学校。

她被转到了特殊护理中心，而且任何可以尝试的办法都被用上了。但是她还是站不起来。她似乎是打不败的。医生们都很喜欢她，并且教她想象自己能成功。每天Angela躺在那儿，虔诚地做脑部练习。

一天，当她又想象自己腿能动的时候，奇迹似乎发生了：床开始动了。她尖叫道：“看，看我在做什么！我成功了！我能动了！我能动了！”

当然，此刻医院里的其他人都感到很害怕。更重要的是，他们是在为了寻求安全而跑。

有的人在哭，设备掉落在地上。你知道了，地震发生了。但是不要把这点告诉Angela。她还以为是自己成功了呢，就像她从来没怀疑过自己能康复一样。几年之后，她又回到了学校。你知道，对于那样一个能晃动地球的人来说，那种疾病难道不就是小问题吗？

21. D 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。本题可以从下文提到的几个线索去考虑：“the doctors said that _____, if any, could come back to no...”, “Having heard this, the little girl...”从这两句就说明她能听也能说，否则就不能和医生交流；还有“her going back to...”，以及后几段里多次出现的“move”一词就知道她不能走动，故应选D。

22. C 【解析】本题考查语境的联系和词义辨析。本题与上一题相辅相成，因为生病所以不能走路，甚至更严重的是根本就不能动，故应选 C。

23. B 【解析】本题考查词的搭配。小女孩非常坚强、执着对自己非常有信心，所以才认为自己康复的机会很大。have/stand a good/fair chance of... 意为“大有…的希望”，所以应选 B。

24. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。小女孩认为自己会康复，但医生却不这么认为，后边说没有任何人，前边应该是说相对好的情况，所以应该是几乎没有。故应选 A。

25. D 【解析】本题考查句意理解。上文医生告诉了她一个不好的消息，通过下文我们知道她并没有因此放弃，而是靠自己的努力创造了奇迹，所以她应该是没有失去信心。satisfied，满意的；delighted，高兴的；surprised，惊讶的；discouraged，失去信心的。所以应选D。

26. A 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。insist，坚持；sigh，叹气；fear，恐惧；promise，承诺。根据句意应该是她坚信自己能复原。所以应选 A。

27. C 【解析】本题考查句意理解。true，真实的；doubtful，令人怀疑的；certain，确定的，肯定的；

impossible,不可能的。根据句意小女孩相信自己能复原，也就是肯定能回到学校。故正确答案为C。

28. B 【解析】本题考查动词短语辨析。get up, 起床；give up, 放弃；turn up, 出现；stand up, 站起来，根据句意应选 B。

29. D 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。disappointed, 失望的；proud, 骄傲的，自豪的；troubled, 困惑的；undefeatable, 无法战胜的，打不败的。句子讲的是小女孩的坚强使得她好像是打不败的。所以应选D。

30. D 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。下文说她每天躺着做脑部练习，也就是想象自己能好起来。所以应选 D。

31. D 【解析】本题考查句意理解。sadly, 悲伤地；madly, 疯狂地；carefully, 认真地；faithfully, 虔诚地。全文都在讲小女孩坚信自己能好起来，所以她做练习的时候肯定是很虔诚，充满信心的。所以应选 D。

32. A 【解析】本题考查连词。根据句意，应该是前后两件事情同时发生，也就是那时候似乎发生了奇迹。所以应选 A。

33. B 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。文章一开始就讲了小女孩的病使她根本就不能动，她坚持不懈地练习就是希望自己能重新站起来，所以这里的奇迹应该是她觉得自己能动了。所以应选 B。

34. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。文章讲她根本不能动，所以 A、D 不正确。B 感到好奇，表示想法，而这是她高兴地说出的话，而且异常激动。所以应选 C。

35. A 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。小女孩感到床在动很高兴，因为她认为自己能动了，而下文告诉我们这是由于地震，地震发生时大家当然会很害怕。

36. C 【解析】本题考查介词。应该是人们为了寻求安全而跑，表示目的。所以应选 C。

37. B 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。rise，上升；fall，掉落；miss，想念，错过；gather，收集。根据句意应该是设备掉落在地上。所以应选 B。

38. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。句意是不要把这件事情告诉小女孩。所以应选 A。

39. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。notice，注意到；suppose，假设；believe，相信；discover，发现。由于小女孩不知道是地震，所以相信自己能动。所以应选 C。

40. C 【解析】本题考查句意理解。push，推；shock，使震惊；shake，晃动；save，挽救。文章讲到那次地震使她觉得自己能动了，就好像她晃动了地球一样。所以应选 C。

##### Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Text 1

【文章概述】本文首先讲到人们对健康的认识状况和社会对于健康和治疗疾病所起的作用，然后从社会学方面讨论了对药的看法。

41. B 【解析】判断题。可以把四个选项逐一与短文第一段进行对照。A 与第三句内容不符；B 与第一、二句的内容相符；C 与第四句的内容不符；D 与第五句的内容不符。所以应选 B。

42. B 【解析】语义题。该词出现在第二段第三句，每个社会对于健康和疾病都有自己的观念，并且给予人们权力来决定谁生病了以及如何给病人治疗。根据该词所跟的宾语看，B符合题意，意思是“给予某人权力”。所以应选B。

43. A 【解析】细节题。根据短文第二段第五句，对于社会学家来说，药是保持人们身体健康和治疗疾病的体系。所以应选 A。

44. C 【解析】判断题。仔细阅读该题，对所给的选项逐一对照，不难看出 A、B 在第三句中被提及，D 在第二句中被提及。只有 C 的内容文章未提及。所以应选 C。

45. A 【解析】主旨题。纵观全文，作者用很大的篇幅，从社会学方面讨论了对待药的看法。所以应选 A。

##### Text 2

【文章概述】本文讲述了信用卡的产生、发展过程。

46. A 【解析】细节题。文章第一句就指出美国从1949年开始盛行使用信用卡。所以应选A。

47. B 【解析】细节题。文章第四段第一句 “The first to turn a profit was Bank of America’s Bank Americard.” 告诉我们这家银行是最先开始盈利的。所以应选 B。

48. A 【解析】判断题。B、C、D都可以在原文中找到，因此是正确的。文章第一段指出美国从1949年开始盛行使用信用卡，但这之前已经开始使用其他种类的卡。所以应选A。

49. A 【解析】语义题。这段讲了这场灾难后开始了一项运动，也就是这场运动由此造成。所以应选 A。

50. A 【解析】确定标题题。统观全文，讲的都是信用卡，其他各项都是其中的细节。所以应选 A。

##### Text 3

【文章概述】本文讲述了一次发生在达拉华河上由华盛顿带领的军队打败英国军队的具体战役。

51. B 【解析】细节题。文章讲的是达拉华河上的一次具体战役，其他选项都太大了。所以应选 B。

52. B 【解析】细节题。文章第二段告诉我们当英国人在12月初到达东岸的时候，华盛顿带领的军队已经安全地过河了，而英国人只能在一个小池塘里找到两艘小船可见英国人是因为没船渡河才在那里安营的。所以应选B。

53. C 【解析】细节题。文章最后一段讲了华盛顿选择圣诞夜进攻的原因：期盼英国军队那时还在庆祝节日。所以应选 C。

54. A 【解析】细节题。文章最后一句点明华盛顿取得了他所需要的胜利。所以应选 A。

55. B 【解析】细节题。根据“In a battle that lasted less than two hours, they defeated the enemy force.” 可知战斗持续了不到两小时。所以应选 B。

##### Text 4

【文章概述】本文对“看法”一词的使用进行了论述，指出它用在不同场合时含义和严肃程度都会有所不同，而且很多时候它只代表一种个人喜好。

56. A 【解析】细节题。文章最后一段第一句就告诉我们作者认为人人都有坚持己见的权利，而且应得到保障。故应选 A。

57. B 【解析】语义理解题。文章第一段最后一句说：谁若质疑他人的看法就很可能会被认为“不能容人”。由此可知道应选 B。

58. C 【解析】理解题。举这个例子是为了说明：“争论个人喜好毫无意义。”也就是不要质疑别人的个人好恶。故应选 C。

59. D 【解析】细节题。文章第三段举例说明了谨慎的判断是建立在深思熟虑的基础上的，这也是它与个人好恶的区别。所以应选 D。

60. A 【解析】语义理解题。文章最后一段指出，坚持己见就是不顾别人的看法按自己的意志行事。所以应选 A。

61.【译文】尽管亨利·福特的名字和大生产的概念相连，但他在劳工保护方面得到了同样的赞誉，因为他早在1913年便实行了劳工保护方面的措施，这些措施用今天的标准来衡量依然是先进的。【解析】这是一个含有让步状语从句的复合句，而且主句中含有一个定语从句。

62.【译文】为了适应更短的日工作时间，整个工厂从双班变成了三班。【解析】这是一个含有目的状语的简单句。

63.【译文】福特汽车公司是最早建立技术学校来培训专门技工和为移民开设英语学校的工厂之一。

##### Text 2

【文章概述】本文讲述了信用卡的产生、发展过程。

46. A 【解析】细节题。文章第一句就指出美国从1949年开始盛行使用信用卡。所以应选A。

47. B 【解析】细节题。文章第四段第一句 “The first to turn a profit was Bank of America’s Bank Americard.” 告诉我们这家银行是最先开始盈利的。所以应选 B。

48. A 【解析】判断题。B、C、D都可以在原文中找到，因此是正确的。文章第一段指出美国从1949年开始盛行使用信用卡，但这之前已经开始使用其他种类的卡。所以应选A。

49. A 【解析】语义题。这段讲了这场灾难后开始了一项运动，也就是这场运动由此造成。所以应选 A。

50. A 【解析】确定标题题。统观全文，讲的都是信用卡，其他各项都是其中的细节。所以应选 A。

##### Text 3

【文章概述】本文讲述了一次发生在达拉华河上由华盛顿带领的军队打败英国军队的具体战役。

55. B 【解析】细节题。根据“In a battle that lasted less than two hours, they defeated the enemy force.” 可知战斗持续了不到两小时。所以应选 B。

##### Text 4

60. A 【解析】语义理解题。文章最后一段指出，坚持己见就是不顾别人的看法按自己的意志行事。所以应选 A。

##### Part B

61.【译文】尽管亨利·福特的名字和大生产的概念相连，但他在劳工保护方面得到了同样的赞誉，因为他早在1913年便实行了劳工保护方面的措施，这些措施用今天的标准来衡量依然是先进的。【解析】这是一个含有让步状语从句的复合句，而且主句中含有一个定语从句。

62.【译文】为了适应更短的日工作时间，整个工厂从双班变成了三班。【解析】这是一个含有目的状语的简单句。

63.【译文】福特汽车公司是最早建立技术学校来培训专门技工和为移民开设英语学校的工厂之一。

【解析】这是一个简单句，只是有一个很长的定语是由不定式充当的。

64.【译文】最受广泛称赞的革新是实行五美元一天的最低工资。其目的是招收和留住那些最好的技工并阻碍工会的发展。【解析】这是一个含有表语从句的复合句，而且表语从句中又含有一个that引导的定语从句。

65.【译文】虽然有人批评福特过多地干涉了员工的私人生活，但毫无疑问，在移民们被用恶劣的方式剥削的时代，亨利·福特却帮助了许多人在美国扎下根来。【解析】这是一个含有让步状语从句的复合句，而且主句中含有一个同位语从句以说明 doubt 的内容。

##### Section IV Writing

##### 解题步骤：

第一步：审题

1. 题型：漫画作文

2. 文体：议论文

第二步：框架分析

1. 一般文章格式

2. 点出每个要点

第三步：列出写作思路

1. 说明图画含义

2. 加以引申

3. 总结自己的看法

第四步：列出关键词和词组

as can be seen in the picture; learning burden is too great; just like birds in cages; all they have to do is study; both in school days and on holiday; attend various additional classes; lead to various problems; have no time of their own; lose the ability to arrange their own time; become tired of learning; give students their free time back, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

As can be seen in the picture, nowadays students' learning burden is too great, just like birds in cages. They lose their valuable freedom, can't do what they want to do. All they have to do is study, both in school days and on holiday. In school days they have to work hard at school, while on holiday they still have no free time, because they have to attend various additional classes.

Such treatment to children will lead to various problems. They have no time of their own, then gradually they will lose the ability to arrange their own time. Everything has to be arranged by others. And because they are always learning, someday they may become tired of learning. And this will make creativity has no place to display.

I think we will give students their free time back. We should allow them the time to fly freely in the sky. So whether attending additional classes or not should be up to their own choices. No one should decide everything for them, treating them just as birds in the cages.

#### 模拟试题（四）

##### Section I Listening Comprehension

1. job 【解析】细节题。根据“I’m fed up with my job”可知正确答案。

2. translators 【解析】细节题。根据 “The European Space Agency is recruiting translators.” 可知正确答案。

3. experience 【解析】细节题。根据“I have plenty of experience”可知正确答案。

4. communication 【解析】细节题。根据“effective communication skills”可知正确答案。

5. negotiable 【解析】细节题。根据 “It’s said to be negotiable.” 可知正确答案。

#### 模拟试题（四）

##### Section I Listening Comprehension

##### Part A

1. job 【解析】细节题。根据“I’m fed up with my job”可知正确答案。

2. translators 【解析】细节题。根据 “The European Space Agency is recruiting translators.” 可知正确答案。

3. experience 【解析】细节题。根据“I have plenty of experience”可知正确答案。

4. communication 【解析】细节题。根据“effective communication skills”可知正确答案。

5. negotiable 【解析】细节题。根据 “It’s said to be negotiable.” 可知正确答案。

6. an advanced economy 【解析】细节题。根据 “We’re now witnessing the emergence of an advanced economy” 可知正确答案。

7. information and technology 【解析】细节题。根据“… an advanced economy based on information and knowledge.”可知正确答案。

8. knowledge 【解析】细节题。根据“... the vital raw material in our economy is knowledge.”可知正确答案。

9. muscles 【解析】细节题。根据“… individuals entering the workforce offer their knowledge, not their muscles.”可知正确答案。

10. mind work 【解析】细节题。根据“… Currently, there out of four jobs involve some form of mind work”可知正确答案。

11. A 【解析】细节题。根据“I was a marketing consultant...”可知本题答案为A。

12. B 【解析】细节题。对话说她的日语是日本之行的优势，可见本题答案为 B。

13. D 【解析】细节题。对话最后说她喜欢坐火车旅行，所以才对新工作感兴趣，由此可知本题答案为D。

14. A 【解析】细节题。文章开头说到她父亲是一位成功的银行投资家，故正确答案为 A。

15. D 【解析】细节题。根据“… in his father's newspaper”可知正确答案为D。

16. C 【解析】推断题。从文章最后一句话与 C 意思一致可推断出正确答案为 C。

17. A 【解析】推断题。根据文章第一句可知正确答案为 A。

18. B 【解析】细节题。根据潜水比航空的费用低, 可知本题答案为 B。

19. D 【解析】细节题。文章指出潜水与航空的相同处是同样的危险，可知本题答案为D。

20. A 【解析】主旨题。作者写这篇文章是为了解释自己兴趣转移的原因，这在文章开头就已讲到，可得出本题选 A。

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

W: Oh, I'm fed up with my job.

M: Hey, there is a perfect job for you in the paper today. You might be interested.

W: Oh, what is it? What do they want?

M: Wait a minute. Er, here it is. The European Space Agency is recruiting translators.

W: The European Space Agency?

M: Well, that's what it says. They need an English translator to work for French or German.

W: So they need a degree in French or German, I suppose. Well, I've got that. What's more, I have plenty of experience. What else are they asking for?

M: Just that. A university degree and three or four years of experience as a translator in a professional environment. They also say the person should have a lively and enquiring mind, effective communication skills and the ability to work individually or as a part of the team.

W: Well, if I stay at my present job much longer, I won't have any mind or skills left. By the way, what about salary? I just hope it isn't lower than what I get now.

M: It's said to be negotiable. It depends on the applicant's education and experience. In addition to basic salary, there is a list of extra benefits. Have a look yourself.

W: Hmm, travel and social security plus relocation expenses are paid. Hey, this isn't bad. I really want the job.

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

W: Oh, I'm fed up with my job.

M: Hey, there is a perfect job for you in the paper today. You might be interested.

W: Oh, what is it? What do they want?

M: Wait a minute. Er, here it is. The European Space Agency is recruiting translators.

W: The European Space Agency?

M: Well, that's what it says. They need an English translator to work for French or German.

W: So they need a degree in French or German, I suppose. Well, I've got that. What's more, I have plenty of experience. What else are they asking for?

M: Just that. A university degree and three or four years of experience as a translator in a professional environment. They also say the person should have a lively and enquiring mind, effective communication skills and the ability to work individually or as a part of the team.

W: Well, if I stay at my present job much longer, I won't have any mind or skills left. By the way, what about salary? I just hope it isn't lower than what I get now.

M: It's said to be negotiable. It depends on the applicant's education and experience. In addition to basic salary, there is a list of extra benefits. Have a look yourself.

W: Hmm, travel and social security plus relocation expenses are paid. Hey, this isn't bad. I really want the job.

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

We’re now witnessing the emergence of an advanced economy based on information and knowledge. Physical labor, raw materials, and capital are no longer the key ingredients in the creation of wealth. Now, the vital raw material in our economy is knowledge. Tomorrow’s wealth depends on the development and exchange of knowledge. And individuals entering the workforce offer their knowledge, not their muscles. Knowledge workers get paid for their education and their ability to learn. Knowledge worker engage in mind work. They deal with symbols: words, figures, and data.

What does all this mean for you? As a future knowledge worker, you can expect to be generating, as well as exchanging information. Currently, there out of four jobs involve some form of mind work, and that number will increase sharply in the future. Management and employees alike will be making decisions in such areas as product development, quality control, and customer satisfaction.

In the new world of work, you can look forward to being in constant training to acquire new skills that will help you keep up with improved technologies and procedures. You can also expect to be taking greater control of your career. Gone are the nine-to-five jobs, lifetime security, predictable promotions, and even the conventional workplace, as you are familiar with. Don't expect the companies will provide you with a clearly defined career path.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

M: Miss Jones, could you tell me more about first job with hotel marketing concepts?

W: Yes, certainly. I was a marketing consultant, responsible for marketing ten UK hotels. They were all luxury hotels in the leisure sector, all of a very high standard.

M: Which markets were you responsible for?

W: For Europe and Japan.

M: I see from your resume that you speak Japanese. Have you ever been to Japan?

W: Yes, I have. I spent a month in Japan in 2006. I met all the key people in the tourist industry, the big tour operators and tourist organizations. As I speak Japanese, I had a very big advantage.

M: Yes, of course. Have you had any contact with Japan in your present job?

W: Yes, I've had a lot. The truth is I have become very popular with the Japanese, both for holidays and for business conferences. In fact, the market for all types of luxury holidays for the Japanese has increased a lot recently.

M: Really, I'm interested to hear more about that, but first, tell me, have you ever traveled on a luxury train? The Orient Express, for example.

W: No I haven't, but I have traveled on a glacier express to Switzerland and I traveled China by train about 8 years ago. I love train travel. That's why I'm very interested in this job.

听第 4 段材料，回答第 14 至 16 题。

Catherine Gram graduated from the University of Chicago in 1938 and got a job as a news reporter in San Francisco. Catherine's father used to be a successful investment banker. In 1933, he bought a failing newspaper, The Washington Post.

Then Catherine returned to Washington and got a job, editing letters in her father's newspaper. She married Philip Gram, who took over his father-in-law's position shortly after and became a publisher of The Washington Post. But for many years, her husband suffered from mental illness and he killed himself in 1963. After her husband's death, Catherine operated the newspaper. In the 1970s, the newspaper became famous around the world and Catherine was also recognized as an important leader in newspaper publishing. She was the first woman to head a major American publishing company, the Washington Post company. In a few years, she successfully expanded the company to include newspaper, magazine, broadcast and cable companies.

She died of head injuries after a fall when she was 84. More than 3 thousand people attended her

### 参考答案与解析

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

We’re now witnessing the emergence of an advanced economy based on information and knowledge. Physical labor, raw materials, and capital are no longer the key ingredients in the creation of wealth. Now, the vital raw material in our economy is knowledge. Tomorrow’s wealth depends on the development and exchange of knowledge. And individuals entering the workforce offer their knowledge, not their muscles. Knowledge workers get paid for their education and their ability to learn. Knowledge worker engage in mind work. They deal with symbols: words, figures, and data.

What does all this mean for you? As a future knowledge worker, you can expect to be generating, as well as exchanging information. Currently, there out of four jobs involve some form of mind work, and that number will increase sharply in the future. Management and employees alike will be making decisions in such areas as product development, quality control, and customer satisfaction.

In the new world of work, you can look forward to being in constant training to acquire new skills that will help you keep up with improved technologies and procedures. You can also expect to be taking greater control of your career. Gone are the nine-to-five jobs, lifetime security, predictable promotions, and even the conventional workplace, as you are familiar with. Don't expect the companies will provide you with a clearly defined career path.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

M: Miss Jones, could you tell me more about first job with hotel marketing concepts?

W: Yes, certainly. I was a marketing consultant, responsible for marketing ten UK hotels. They were all luxury hotels in the leisure sector, all of a very high standard.

M: Which markets were you responsible for?

W: For Europe and Japan.

M: I see from your resume that you speak Japanese. Have you ever been to Japan?

W: Yes, I have. I spent a month in Japan in 2006. I met all the key people in the tourist industry, the big tour operators and tourist organizations. As I speak Japanese, I had a very big advantage.

M: Yes, of course. Have you had any contact with Japan in your present job?

W: Yes, I've had a lot. The truth is I have become very popular with the Japanese, both for holidays and for business conferences. In fact, the market for all types of luxury holidays for the Japanese has increased a lot recently.

M: Really, I'm interested to hear more about that, but first, tell me, have you ever traveled on a luxury train? The Orient Express, for example.

W: No I haven't, but I have traveled on a glacier express to Switzerland and I traveled China by train about 8 years ago. I love train travel. That's why I'm very interested in this job.

听第 4 段材料，回答第 14 至 16 题。

Catherine Gram graduated from the University of Chicago in 1938 and got a job as a news reporter in San Francisco. Catherine's father used to be a successful investment banker. In 1933, he bought a failing newspaper, The Washington Post.

Then Catherine returned to Washington and got a job, editing letters in her father's newspaper. She married Philip Gram, who took over his father-in-law's position shortly after and became a publisher of The Washington Post. But for many years, her husband suffered from mental illness and he killed himself in 1963. After her husband's death, Catherine operated the newspaper. In the 1970s, the newspaper became famous around the world and Catherine was also recognized as an important leader in newspaper publishing. She was the first woman to head a major American publishing company, the Washington Post company. In a few years, she successfully expanded the company to include newspaper, magazine, broadcast and cable companies.

She died of head injuries after a fall when she was 84. More than 3 thousand people attended her

funeral, including many government and business leaders. Her friends said she would be remembered as a woman who had an important influence on events in the United States and the world. Catherine once wrote, "The world without newspapers would not be the same kind of world." After her death, the employees of The Washington Post wrote, "The world without Catherine would not be the same at all."

听第5段材料，回答第17至20题。

When my interest shifted from space to the sea, I never expected it would cause such confusion among my friends, yet I can understand their feelings. As I have been writing and talking about space flight for the best part of 20 years, a sudden switch of interest to the depth of the sea doer seem peculiar. To explain, I'd like to share my reasons behind this unusual change of mind. The first excuse I give is an economic one. Underwater exploration is so much cheaper than space flight. The first round-trip ticket to the moon is going to cost at least 10 billion dollars if you include research and development. By the end of this century, the cost will be down to a few million. On the other hand, the diving suit and a set of basic tools needed for skin-diving can be bought for 20 dollars. My second argument is more philosophical. The ocean, surprisingly enough, has many things in common with space. In their different ways, both sea and space are equally hostile. If we wish to survive in either for any length of time, we need to have mechanical aids. The diving suit helped the design of the space suit. The feelings and the emotions of a man beneath the sea will be much like those of a man beyond the atmosphere.

##### Section II Use of English

[参考译文] 学习方法有很多。对一些学生有用的方法对其他人可能根本没有帮助。你所能做的事情就是不断尝试直到找到适合你的方法。但是有两件事是确定的：没有人可以为你学习，而且只有找到适合你的方法你才能考上大学。同时，确实有一些适合每个人的办法。也就是“不要落后”。

开始时是很困难的，你几乎不可能在一周内完成三周的工作。即使最快的读者也很难做到这点。如果你迟交了必须交的书面作业，很晚才意识到这点的老师可能不会给你很好的成绩，也可能他根本就不收你的作业。由于在其他的功课上花费了太多的时间而使得某门功课落后确实不是什么借口。感觉精力充沛地学了七小时化学对历史老师的测验一点帮助也没有。而且确实有很多新生遇到了这种困扰，他们在一门功课上花费了太多的时间而导致没有时间学其他的功课。他们应该把所有的时间用在这方面。无论什么原因，全力以赴学习一门功课而忽略了其他的功课就是一种错误。如果你受到了这种诱惑，就从最简单最少的功课开始吧。做完这些再去做更难、更花费时间的功课。

21. D 【解析】本题考查语法。well 是副词，good 是形容词，修饰动词应该用副词，所以 A 不可。与后面相比较的 doesn't work at all for others 可知此处应填一个与 doesn't work at all 意义相反的词，显然 B、C 不能满足这个要求。故应选 D。

22. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。此句的意思是“你一直进行尝试，直到找到适合自己的学习方法”。因而此处的连词要表达“直到”的意思。所以应选 A。

23. B 【解析】本题考查句意理解。解题线索是空格后的 else，两个词连起来表示“除了你自己，别的任何人都不能…”。所以应选 B。

24. D 【解析】本题考查词义的辨析。work 常用于抽象的意义，表示起作用的意思。故应选 D。

25. A 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。get (fall, be) behind 表示落后的意思，根据上下文，显然是说，我们在学习上是不要落后。所以应选 A。

26. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。此处要求填一个形容词，故可排除 A。又根据上下文，可以推理出这句话的意思应该是“开始时是很困难的，你几乎不可能在一周内完成三周的工作。”这里谈的是难度的问题，因此其他的答案是不合适的。所以应选 C。

27. C 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据从句中的意思来判断本题的答案。不管选哪个答案，我们都知道要在一周内做三周的事，毫无疑问，应当说这是几乎不可能的。故正确答案为 C。

Catherine once wrote, "The world without newspapers would not be the same kind of world." 24. D 【解析】本题考查词义的辨析。work 常用于抽象的意义，表示起作用的意思。故应选 D。

25. A 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。get (fall, be) behind 表示落后的意思，根据上下文，显然是说，我们在学习上是不要落后。所以应选 A。

26. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。此处要求填一个形容词，故可排除 A。又根据上下文，可以推理出这句话的意思应该是“开始时是很困难的，你几乎不可能在一周内完成三周的工作。”这里谈的是难度的问题，因此其他的答案是不合适的。所以应选 C。

27. C 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据从句中的意思来判断本题的答案。不管选哪个答案，我们都知道要在一周内做三周的事，毫无疑问，应当说这是几乎不可能的。故正确答案为 C。

28. C 【解析】本题考查名词的数。三周肯定是复数，week 必须是复数形式；work 是不可数名词。故应选 C。

29. A 【解析】本题考查的是篇章词汇。解本题有两种方法：第一种方法从篇章的角度着手，本段承接上面一段，说的是学习的困难，在这里显然是说“即使”是读得最快的人也有困难，选A顺理成章；第二种方法从句法着手，因为这是一个简单句，显然不能填连词和介词，故C、D明显错误。单就本句来说，选almost也是不能的。所以应选A。

30. B 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。have troubling 后要求接动名词，表示在做某事时有困难。所以应选 B。

31. A 【解析】本题考查短语动词的辨析。turn in 的意思是“交上去，上交”，符合文意。turn up 出现；turn out，出来；结果是；given in，让步。

32. D 【解析】本题考查句意理解。此处表示“过晚”之意，用 too。所以应选 D。

33. C 【解析】本题考查句型。“根本不”的英文表达是“not... at all”。所以应选 C。

34. D 【解析】本题考查名词辨析。这一句及其下一句是说因为你在别的科花的时间太多而在另一科落后，前者不是后者的理由。所以此处要选表示“借口、理由”意思的词，即是 excuse。所以应选 D。

35. A 【解析】本题考查动词辨析。表示笼统的“有用”的动词是 help。encourage，鼓励；assist，帮助；improve，提高。所以应选 A。

36. A 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。at the expense of 的意思是“以…为代价”。所以应选 A。

37. A 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。注意后面的介词 to, devote to, 致力于, 是一个固定搭配。所以应选 A。

38. B 【解析】本题考查句型。whatever the reason,无论什么原因，是固定搭配，有时后边会加上 may be。所以应选 B。

39. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。前面所说的明显是一种诱惑，所以要选 temptation。attraction, 吸引；decision, 决定；dilemma, 进退两难的处境。所以应选 C。

40. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。前面说的是上什么什么课的事，所以选 A 项 class。arrangement,安排；way，道路，方法；assignment，作业。

##### Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Text 1

【文章概述】本文先讲到《巴黎条约》签订后英国为保护自己利益所采取的一系列措施，接着讲到《邮票法案》遇到激烈反对并成为美国革命的导火线之一。

41. A 【解析】推断题。由第一段法国对这一大陆的影响结束了可知法国曾经控制过这一地区。所以应选 A。

42. D 【解析】细节题。由第一段“the Grenville Program, passed by Parliament and designed to raise money in the colonies.”可知这是英国议会通过的用来在殖民地筹钱的法案。所以应选D。

43. C 【解析】细节题。文章第一段第三句明确指出《邮票法案》是美国革命的导火线之一。其他几项不全面。所以应选 C。

44. C 【解析】文意理解题。文章第二段指出但是当人们开始讨论《邮票法案》的时候，他几乎是孤军奋战的。也就是说他是一个人在公开反抗。所以应选 C。

45. A 【解析】主旨题。文章主要介绍的是最终导致美国革命的一个事件，B、C、D都是其中的细节。所以应选A。

##### Text 2

【文章概述】本文介绍了睡眠的作用和重要性，并讲述了如何克服失眠。

38. B 【解析】本题考查句型。whatever the reason,无论什么原因，是固定搭配，有时后边会加上 may be。所以应选 B。

39. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。前面所说的明显是一种诱惑，所以要选 temptation。attraction, 吸引；decision, 决定；dilemma, 进退两难的处境。所以应选 C。

40. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。前面说的是上什么什么课的事，所以选 A 项 class。arrangement,安排；way，道路，方法；assignment，作业。

##### Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Part A

##### Text 1

【文章概述】本文先讲到《巴黎条约》签订后英国为保护自己利益所采取的一系列措施，接着讲到《邮票法案》遇到激烈反对并成为美国革命的导火线之一。

41. A 【解析】推断题。由第一段法国对这一大陆的影响结束了可知法国曾经控制过这一地区。所以应选 A。

42. D 【解析】细节题。由第一段“the Grenville Program, passed by Parliament and designed to raise money in the colonies.”可知这是英国议会通过的用来在殖民地筹钱的法案。所以应选D。

43. C 【解析】细节题。文章第一段第三句明确指出《邮票法案》是美国革命的导火线之一。其他几项不全面。所以应选 C。

44. C 【解析】文意理解题。文章第二段指出但是当人们开始讨论《邮票法案》的时候，他几乎是孤军奋战的。也就是说他是一个人在公开反抗。所以应选 C。

45. A 【解析】主旨题。文章主要介绍的是最终导致美国革命的一个事件，B、C、D都是其中的细节。所以应选A。

##### Text 2

【文章概述】本文介绍了睡眠的作用和重要性，并讲述了如何克服失眠。

46. C 【解析】细节题。由第一段第一句可知睡眠会在我们醒来时使我们感到精力充沛。所以应选C。

47. C 【解析】细节题。由第二段第三句可知睡眠策略是因人而异的。所以应选 C。

48. B 【解析】细节题。由第二段最后一句可知每个人都会有失眠的经历。所以应选 B。

49. B 【解析】细节题。由第三段二、三句可知你必须做的第一点就是尽可能地减少压力。所以应选 B。

50. C 【解析】推断题。文章最后一句指出疲劳的身体可能睡得更好。因此可推断出大量运动会睡得更好。所以应选 C。

##### Text 3

[文章概述] 本文作者从几个方面论述了广告的作用和意义。

51. D 【解析】语义理解题。作者指出花在广告上的钱和他所知道的花在其他地方的钱是一样的，也就是作者认为这些钱是值得花的。所以应选D。

52. D 【解析】细节题。文章第一段讲了广告的优点：它能极大地提高人们的生活水平；能确保对劳动力需求的增加，因此是对抗失业极有效的办法；它降低了很多服务的开销。所以A、B、C符合文意，而D项正好与文意相反。所以应选D。

53. D 【解析】推断题。文章第四、五段讲了广告的作用是不能严格区分的，因此可推断出作者认为那位电视人物的观点是片面的。所以应选D。

54. C 【解析】推断题。文章第四、五段讲了广告的作用是不能严格区分的，传递信息的目的也是为了说服消费者购买产品。所以应选 C。

55. B 【解析】观点态度题。文章第一段作者就讲了广告的很多优点，因此可判断作者对广告是持肯定态度的。所以应选 B。

##### Text 4

[文章概述] 本文论述了男女在家庭中获得平等权利，共同承担责任的必要性以及对于孩子的积极作用。

56. A 【解析】细节题。由文章最后一句可知，获得平等权利和共同承担责任的理想与健康的民主和健康的家庭有关，也就是说这是良好的民主的基础。所以应选 A。

57. A 【解析】文意理解题。第二段第一句说到：如果这个运动过于激烈，男性的角色将被认为不那么重要了，而这确实发生过。所以应选 A。

58. D 【解析】细节题。由“bringing up children involves a partnership of equals”可知作者认为抚养孩子需要父母平等参与。所以应选D。

59. C 【解析】推断题。作者认为抚养孩子需要父母平等参与，也就是父亲与孩子的成长也有关系。所以应选 C。

60. A 【解析】推断题。由文章最后一句可以推断出作者会同意健康、合作的家庭是健康社会的基本元素这一观点。所以应选 A。

61.【译文】许多通过征求家人、朋友或同事的意见而解决的生活中的问题，在大家庭中就是力所不及的。【解析】这是一个含有定语从句的复合句，由 that 引导。

62.【译文】由于搬离固有的社区，这些家庭就会与多年的朋友、亲戚失去联系，他们的小道消息来源也被中断了，同时他们也失去了需要时能获得有用信息的信心。【解析】这首先是一个含有原因状语从句的复合句，主句是两个由and连接的并列句。

63.【译文】现代人可以获得比以往任何时代都要多的信息，而且找到与自己的具体问题相关的信

##### Part B

61.【译文】许多通过征求家人、朋友或同事的意见而解决的生活中的问题，在大家庭中就是力所不及的。【解析】这是一个含有定语从句的复合句，由 that 引导。

62.【译文】由于搬离固有的社区，这些家庭就会与多年的朋友、亲戚失去联系，他们的小道消息来源也被中断了，同时他们也失去了需要时能获得有用信息的信心。【解析】这首先是一个含有原因状语从句的复合句，主句是两个由and连接的并列句。

63.【译文】现代人可以获得比以往任何时代都要多的信息，而且找到与自己的具体问题相关的信

息是一个非常复杂、耗时，有时甚至是使人筋疲力尽的事情。【解析】这是一个由 and 连接的并列句。

64.【译文】与持续增加的信息量相伴随的是科技的发展，这使得人们可以以比以往更快的速度存储或把更多的信息传递到更多的地方。【解析】这是一个倒装句。由于主语太长所以放在了后边，而且其中含有一个定语从句。

65.【译文】“知识就是力量”可能更会成为至理名言，而且获取信息的能力成为所有人都必备的条件。【解析】这是一个由and连接的并列句。

##### Section IV Writing

##### 解题步骤：

第一步：审题

1. 题型：话题作文

2. 文体：书信

第二步：框架分析

1. 书信格式

2. 点出每个要点

第三步：列出写作思路

1. 问候、提出写信目的，引出话题 2. 阐述自己对于该问题的观点 3. 结束语 第四步：列出关键词和词组

a heated discussion about examination; hold that examination should be abolished; praise the cancellation of exams; a student who earns good marks is generally a good student; a grade average of the year's work; the best way to choose good students for college; comparatively fair; is the best way to choose talents for our country at present time; it is unpractical to abolish examination, etc.

第五步：落笔成文

##### Dear Mr. Smith,

I have read your recent newspaper, and I see there is a heated discussion about examination. Some hold that examination should be abolished. They write articles to praise the cancellation of exams because they believe “tests don’t tell the whole story.”

As a teacher who has many years' experience of teaching, I have witnessed that a student who earns good marks is generally a good student, and that a student's final mark in a subject is usually a grade average of the year's work. Of course there are exceptions, but they don't have the frequency that give an unfair picture of a student's ability. And in the present situation, examination is the best way to choose good students for colleges and universities. Because it is comparatively fair and can show a student's ability to a great extent. Much evidence has proven that graduates selected in this way have fully displayed their ability and also have shown that examination is the best way to choose talents for our country at present time.

So I think it is unpractical to abolish examination now.

Yours respectfully,

A reader

第五步：落笔成文

##### Dear Mr. Smith,

I have read your recent newspaper, and I see there is a heated discussion about examination. Some hold that examination should be abolished. They write articles to praise the cancellation of exams because they believe “tests don’t tell the whole story.”

As a teacher who has many years' experience of teaching, I have witnessed that a student who earns good marks is generally a good student, and that a student's final mark in a subject is usually a grade average of the year's work. Of course there are exceptions, but they don't have the frequency that give an unfair picture of a student's ability. And in the present situation, examination is the best way to choose good students for colleges and universities. Because it is comparatively fair and can show a student's ability to a great extent. Much evidence has proven that graduates selected in this way have fully displayed their ability and also have shown that examination is the best way to choose talents for our country at present time.

So I think it is unpractical to abolish examination now.

Yours respectfully,

A reader

#### 模拟试题(五)

##### Section I Listening Comprehension

##### Part A

1. health 【解析】细节题。根据 “Obtaining good health insurance is a real necessity while you are studying overseas.” 可知正确答案。

2. savings 【解析】细节题。根据“It protects you from minor and major medical expenses that can

wipe out not only your savings but your dreams of an education abroad."可知正确答案。

3. two 【解析】细节题。根据“There are often two different types of health insurance you can consider buying”可知正确答案。

4. home 【解析】细节题。根据“An international travel insurance is usually purchased in your home country…”可知正确答案。

5. small 【解析】细节题。根据“… getting student health insurance in the country where you will study might allow you to only pay a small percentage of the medical cost…”可知正确答案。

6. in science 【解析】细节题。根据 “Teamwork is just as important in science as it is on the playing field or in the gym.” 可知正确答案。

7. group work 【解析】细节题。根据“Group work is necessary …”可知正确答案。

8. deserve consideration 【解析】细节题。根据“What comes first is to keep an open mind, because everyone’s ideas deserve consideration...”可知正确答案。

9. own contribution 【解析】细节题。根据“... each group member can make his or her own contribution.”可知正确答案。

10. by small groups 【解析】细节题。根据“Activities like investigations are most effective when done by small groups.”可知正确答案。

11. C 【解析】细节题。根据“I know that you have been robbed.”可知本题答案为 C。

12. A 【解析】细节题。根据女士说她被绑起来了，可知本题答案为 A。

13. D 【解析】细节题。根据女士说她钱包里有200美元，可知本题答案为D。

14. D 【解析】推断题。文章最后一句说司机刚从超市出来，由此可推断出正确答案为D。

15. D 【解析】推断题。根据“… he had no one to blame. He was so busy… he didn't get the name of the driver of the sports car”可推断出正确答案为D。

16. C 【解析】细节题。听完全文可知并没有人受伤，只是保罗的车受损了，因此可知正确答案为 C。

17. D 【解析】细节题。听完全文可知这是盗窃案，因此正确答案为D。

18. A 【解析】细节题。根据“... especially if they flash a card to you.”可知本题答案为 A。

19. A 【解析】细节题。警察警告人们如果没有预约不要让任何人进入自己的家，因此本题答案为A。

20. A 【解析】细节题。文章指出老人是去邮局领养老金的，结果都被偷了，由此可知本题选 A。

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

Obtaining good health insurance is a real necessity while you are studying overseas. It protects you from minor and major medical expenses that can wipe out not only your savings but your dreams of an education abroad. There are often two different types of health insurance you can consider buying, international travel insurance and student insurance in the country where you will be going.

An international travel insurance is usually purchased in your home country before you go abroad. It generally covers a wide variety of medical services and you are often given a list of doctors in the area where you will travel who may even speak your native language. The drawback might be that you may not get your money back immediately, in other words, you may have to pay all you medical expenses and then later submit your receipts to the insurance company.

On the other hand, getting student health insurance in the country where you will study might allow you to only pay a small percentage of the medical cost at the time of the service and thus you don't

7. group work 【解析】细节题。根据“Group work is necessary …”可知正确答案。

9. own contribution 【解析】细节题。根据“... each group member can make his or her own contribution.”可知正确答案。

10. by small groups 【解析】细节题。根据“Activities like investigations are most effective when done by small groups.”可知正确答案。

##### Part C

11. C 【解析】细节题。根据“I know that you have been robbed.”可知本题答案为C。

12. A 【解析】细节题。根据女士说她被绑起来了，可知本题答案为 A。

13. D 【解析】细节题。根据女士说她钱包里有200美元，可知本题答案为D。

14. D 【解析】推断题。文章最后一句说司机刚从超市出来，由此可推断出正确答案为 D。

15. D 【解析】推断题。根据“… he had no one to blame. He was so busy… he didn't get the name of the driver of the sports car”可推断出正确答案为D。

16. C 【解析】细节题。听完全文可知并没有人受伤，只是保罗的车受损了，因此可知正确答案为 C。

17. D 【解析】细节题。听完全文可知这是盗窃案，因此正确答案为D。

18. A 【解析】细节题。根据“… especially if they flash a card to you.”可知本题答案为 A。

19. A 【解析】细节题。警察警告人们如果没有预约不要让任何人进入自己的家，因此本题答案为A。

20. A 【解析】细节题。文章指出老人是去邮局领养老金的，结果都被偷了，由此可知本题选 A。

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

Obtaining good health insurance is a real necessity while you are studying overseas. It protects you from minor and major medical expenses that can wipe out not only your savings but your dreams of an education abroad. There are often two different types of health insurance you can consider buying, international travel insurance and student insurance in the country where you will be going.

An international travel insurance is usually purchased in your home country before you go abroad. It generally covers a wide variety of medical services and you are often given a list of doctors in the area where you will travel who may even speak your native language. The drawback might be that you may not get your money back immediately, in other words, you may have to pay all you medical expenses and then later submit your receipts to the insurance company.

On the other hand, getting student health insurance in the country where you will study might allow you to only pay a small percentage of the medical cost at the time of the service and thus you don't

have to have sufficient cash to pay the entire bill at once. Whatever you decide, obtaining some form of health insurance is something you should consider before you go overseas. You shouldn't wait until you are sick with a major medical bills to pay off.

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

Teamwork is just as important in science as it is on the playing field or in the gym. Scientific investigations are almost always carried out by teams of people working together. Ideas are shared, experiences are designed, data are analyzed, and results are evaluated and shared with other investigators. Group work is necessary, and is usually more productive than working alone.

Several times throughout the year you may be asked to work with one or more of your classmates. Whatever the task your group is assigned, a few rules need to be followed to ensure a productive and successful experience.

What comes first is to keep an open mind, because everyone's ideas deserve consideration and each group member can make his or her own contribution. Secondly, it makes a job easier to divide the group task among all group members. Choose a role on the team that is best suited to your particular strengths. Thirdly, always work together, take turns, and encourage each other by listening, clarifying, and trusting one another. Mutual support and trust often make a great difference.

Activities like investigations are most effective when done by small groups. Here are some more suggestions for effective team performance during these activities: Make sure each group member understands and agrees to the task given to him or her, and everyone knows exactly when, why and what to do; take turns doing various tasks during similar and repeated activities; be aware of where other group members are and what they are doing so as to ensure safety; be responsible for your own learning, though it is by no means unwise to compare your observations with those of other group members.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

M: Good morning, madam. I know that you have been robbed.

W: Yes, I have.

M: When did this happen?

W: About two hours ago.

M: Why didn't you report it before?

W: I couldn't. I was bound and gagged.

M: Would you like to tell me what happened?

W: Yes. I was sitting in this room watching television when someone knocked at the door. Without thinking, I opened it.

M: That wasn't very wise, madam.

W: I know. I was expecting my husband, you see, and I thought it was him.

M: You should never open a door without looking to see who it is.

W: Yes, I know. I regret it very much.

M: What happened?

W: Two men pushed me into the flat. One of them threatened me with a knife while the other bound and gagged me.

M: Did you get a good look at them?

W: I'm afraid not. They were both wearing stockings over their faces.

M: What did they take?

W: My wallet, which has 200 dollars in it, my wristwatch, some of my jewelry from our bedroom, and a silver photo frame.

M: They don't seem to have done much damage to the flat.

What comes first is to keep an open mind, because everyone's ideas deserve consideration and each group member can make his or her own contribution. Secondly, it makes a job easier to divide the group task among all group members. Choose a role on the team that is best suited to your particular strengths. Thirdly, always work together, take turns, and encourage each other by listening, clarifying, and trusting one another. Mutual support and trust often make a great difference.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

M: Good morning, madam. I know that you have been robbed.

W: Yes, I have.

M: When did this happen?

W: About two hours ago.

M: Why didn't you report it before?

W: I couldn't. I was bound and gagged.

M: Would you like to tell me what happened?

W: Yes. I was sitting in this room watching television when someone knocked at the door. Without thinking, I opened it.

M: That wasn't very wise, madam.

W: I know. I was expecting my husband, you see, and I thought it was him.

M: You should never open a door without looking to see who it is.

W: Yes, I know. I regret it very much.

M: What happened?

W: Two men pushed me into the flat. One of them threatened me with a knife while the other bound and gagged me.

M: Did you get a good look at them?

W: I'm afraid not. They were both wearing stockings over their faces.

M: What did they take?

W: My wallet, which has 200 dollars in it, my wristwatch, some of my jewelry from our bedroom, and a silver photo frame.

M: They don't seem to have done much damage to the flat.

W: No. They had just begun to search when the dogs next door began to bark. They ran off quickly leaving me bound and gagged. It was some time before my husband returned and released me. I phoned the police at once.

M: The policeman began searching the area as soon as we received your call. I can't promise you that we'll recover your property but we'll certainly do our best.

听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

Paul, a salesman from London, was driving past a sports car parked outside a supermarket, when he saw it start to roll slowly down the hill. Inside the car were two young girls on the passenger seat — but no driver. Paul stopped quickly, jumped in front of the sports car and tried to stop it, pushing against the front of the car. Another man who was standing nearby got into the car and put on the handbrake, saving the girls from injury.

It was at this point that Paul noticed his own car rolling slowly down the hill and going too fast for him to stop it. It crashed into a bus at the bottom of the hill and was so badly damaged that it had to be pulled away to a garage.

As if this was not bad enough, Paul now found he had no one to blame. He was so busy chasing his car that he didn't get the name of the driver of the sports car, who just came out of the supermarket and drove away without realizing what had happened.

听第5段材料，回答第17至20题。

W: Gosh! Have you seen this, Richard?

M: Seen what?

W: In the paper, it says there's a man going round pretending he's from the electricity board. He's been calling at people's home, saying he's come to check that all their appliances are safe. Then he gets around them to make him a cup of tea and while they are out of the room, he steals their money, handbag, whatever and makes off with it.

M: But you know Jane, it's partly their own fault. You should never let anyone like that in unless you are expecting them.

W: It's all very well to say that, but someone comes to the door and says electricity or gas, and you automatically think they are OK, especially if they flash a card to you.

M: Does this man have an ID then?

W: Yes, that's just it! It seems he used to work for the electricity board at one time. According to the paper, the police are warning people, especially pensioners not to admit anyone unless they have an appointment. It's a bit sad. One old lady told them she'd just been to the post office to draw her pension when he called. She said he must have followed her home. He stole the whole lot.

M: But what does he look like? Surely they must have a description.

W: Oh, yes, they have. Let's see. In his thirties, tall, bushy dark hair, slight northern accent, sounds a bit like you actually.

##### Section II Use of English

[参考译文] 旅游既有趣又容易。当旅游条件好的时候去别的国家旅游尤其令人高兴。良好的旅游条件包括舒适的交通方式，掌握那个国家的语言，熟悉那个国家的风俗习惯和令人愉悦的旅游伙伴。我们都有过像这样的美好旅行。

同时我们很多人也有我们希望忘掉的旅游经历。很多糟糕的情况可能带来不好的旅游经历。例如，如果我们上边列举的四种情况不存在，我们很可能就会有不愉快的经历。或者最好的情况也会是艰难的旅游。去国外学习的学生就可能有这种艰难的旅程。他们多是独自出行，而且还不懂新国家的语言。他们到达这个新国家的第一站通常是在一个国际机场。他们需要找

到从机场到学校的路。他们可能还要换航班，坐公交车、火车或出租车。他们需要在一个还不熟悉的国家做所有这些事情。过后，当旅行结束的时候，他们会笑。但在当时他们感到很糟糕。

21. B 【解析】本题考查形容词辨析。happy，高兴的，主语指人或有生命的物体的情绪；enjoyable，令人感到高兴的，主语一般是物；amusing，令人感到可笑的；favorable，有利的。故应选B。

22. A 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。根据句意这里讲的应该是好的旅游条件包括哪些方面，include，包括；conclude，达成；hold，拿着；contain，包含，一般指全部的事物。所以应选 A。

23. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。文章开头就告诉我们假期去别的国家旅游令人感到愉快，所以应选 C。

24. D 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。熟悉某物的英文表达是“familiar with, familiarity with”, 故应选 D。

25. A 【解析】本题考查名词辨析。根据句意应该是旅游伙伴，A 伙伴，同伴；B 朋友；C 同志；D 家伙，人。所以应选 A。

26. C 【解析】本题考查介词。根据句意应该是像这样的旅行，故 A、D 不正确。as 后边应该加从句或者介词短语，like 后边加名词或代词。所以应选 C。

27. C 【解析】本题考查句意的理解。句意应该是希望忘掉，故正确答案为 C。

28. B 【解析】本题考查上下文的理解。通篇都是讲的旅游，所以 C、D 不正确。trip 不能作形容词表示旅游。故应选 B。

29. D 【解析】本题考查句意的理解。本句指的是上边列举的几种情况，故 A、C 不正确，number 指标出序号，不符合题意。所以应选 D。

30. A 【解析】本题考查连词。上边提了一种情况，下边又说了一种选择。所以应选A。

31. B 【解析】本题考查代词。上边提到了一种旅游情况，这里提到的是另一种，所以用代词来指代以避免重复。所以应选 B。

32. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。全文都在讲去国外的旅行。所以应选 C。

33. C 【解析】本题考查语法。修饰动词“出行”应该用副词，故 A、B、D 不正确，因为它们都是形容词。所以应选 C。

34. A 【解析】本题考查语法。B 应用在否定句句尾；C 一般用在肯定句句尾或句中，而且从句意上来说用在这里也不正确；D 表示肯定意义。根据句意此处应该是否定的意思：还没有掌握语言。所以应选 A。

35. A 【解析】本题考查介词。在机场应该用 at。所以应选 A。

36. D 【解析】本题考查句意理解。此处应该是指找到去学校的路。所以应选D。

37. D 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。drift, 漂移, 动词; exchange, 交换; board, 登(飞机、车、船等)动词; change, 换, 换乘。所以应选 D。

38. A 【解析】本题考查语法。这是一个定语从句，前边的先行词是地点，所以应该用表示地点的关系代词。所以应选 A。

39. B 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据文意，此处应该是指这种状况结束的时候，但要注意它不是被动地、人为地结束，而是按计划自然结束，故 A、C、D 不正确。be over，结束。所以应选 B。

40. B 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。date, 日期；time, 时间，一段时期；while, 一般和 a 连用表示一会儿；day, 天。根据句意此处应该是指那段时间。所以应选 B。

39. B 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据文意，此处应该是指这种状况结束的时候，但要注意它不是被动地、人为地结束，而是按计划自然结束，故 A、C、D 不正确。be over，结束。所以应选 B。

40. B 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。date，日期；time，时间，一段时期；while，一般和a连用表示一会儿；day，天。根据句意此处应该是指那段时间。所以应选B。

# Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Part A

##### Text 1

【文章概述】本文对感冒做了较全面的介绍，包括传播途径、易感人群、预防及缓解办法。

41. B 【解析】细节题。由第一段最后一句话就可以知道感冒是由病毒感染引起的。所以应选 B。

42. A 【解析】细节题。第五段第一句就指出预防感冒最有效的办法就是保持个人卫生，而 B 只是

保持个人卫生的具体办法。所以应选 A。

43. B 【解析】细节题。由第二段最后一句可知免疫力低是儿童容易感冒的原因。所以应选 B。

44. C 【解析】细节题。由第三段可知人每次感冒时不可能由同一种病毒引起。所以应选 C。

45. D 【解析】常识题。根据常识我们都知道感冒的常见症状。所以应选D。

##### Text 2

[文章概述] 本文主要讲现在的书与以前的书在很多方面都发生了改变，现在的人们也更喜欢读书了。

46. D 【解析】语义题。根据后边的内容可知现在的书与以前不同了，也就是发生了改变。因此应选D。

47. D 【解析】理解题。由第二段第一句就可知这段主要讲的是关于孩子喜欢的书，因为这是这段的中心句。所以应选D。

48. C 【解析】判断题。由第三段第一句可知传统的儿童读物并没有消失。所以应选 C。

49. D 【解析】语义题。这句话的意思是说要向儿童传递这些信息，而 D 也是向某人传递信息的意思。所以应选 D。

50. C 【解析】主旨题。这一段第一句就指出公众热情地迎接着出版商展示的丰富创造力。而D是反映强烈的意思，不如C恰当。所以应选C。

##### Text 3

【文章概述】本文介绍了英国的等级制度以及处在不同等级的人在口音、社会地位、受教育程度方面的差异。

51. D 【解析】细节题。文章第一段的倒数第二句指出中产阶级主要包括富裕的生意人和有一定职业的人。所以应选D。

52. C 【解析】细节题。文章第二段的第一句指出他们之间最明显的区别是他们的口音。所以应选C。

53. A 【解析】推断题。文章的最后两句指出谦卑会让人感到尴尬与厌烦。甚至“先生”这个词，除了在学校和一些特定的职业(例如，商业、军队等等)外，也听起来很谦卑而不经常使用。因此可推断出正确答案为 A。

54. C 【解析】细节题。文章第一段告诉我们上层阶级里包括世袭贵族阶级。所以应选 C。

55. B 【解析】细节题。文章第三段第二句的前半句指出来自工人阶级家庭的学生接受大学教育并且获得某种职业的情况已十分普遍。而 B 与此不符。所以应选 B。

##### Text 4

【文章概述】本文讲了标准语、俗语和俚语在使用和收录方面的区别，并介绍了一些语言学家关于俚语的形成的一些看法。

56. C 【解析】细节题。从第一段就可以知道语言大致可以分为标准语、俗语和俚语这三种，因此正确答案为 C。

57. C 【解析】细节题。根据第一段最后一句 “Both colloquial usage and slang are more common in speech than in writing.” 可知 C 为正确答案，其他的表述与原文正好相反。

58. D 【解析】细节题。第三段已经明确提到了 A、B、C 三项所指的内容，只有 D 没有被提到，因此正确答案为 D。

59. D 【解析】语义理解题。根据画线句子后面的一句可知大部分语言使用者都不知道他们使用的是俗语还是俚语，也就是大部分人不会意识到他们自己使用的是哪种语言，因此正确答案是D。

56. C 【解析】细节题。从第一段就可以知道语言大致可以分为标准语、俗语和俚语这三种，因此正确答案为 C。

58. D 【解析】细节题。第三段已经明确提到了 A、B、C 三项所指的内容，只有 D 没有被提到，因此正确答案为 D。

59. D 【解析】语义理解题。根据画线句子后面的一句可知大部分语言使用者都不知道他们使用的是俗语还是俚语，也就是大部分人不会意识到他们自己使用的是哪种语言，因此正确答案是D。

60. A 【解析】确定标题题。这篇文章主要讲了语言的种类，因此正确答案为 A。

61.【译文】美国的经济体系基本上是按照私人企业、市场经济的模式建立的。在这种经济体制下，消费者的消费和他们所最需要的服务在很大程度上决定了产品的生产。【解析】这是一个含有定语从句的复合句，由 in which 引导。定语从句中又含有一个宾语从句和定语从句。

62.【译文】在美国的经济体制下，与对个体消费者的需求相伴随的是商人获得最大利润的愿望和个体消费者获得最多收入的愿望。这二者共同决定了产品的生产和资源的利用方式。【解析】这是一个强调句型。

63.【译文】在美国经济中，这种机制是由价格体系来调节的。在这一过程，价格取决于消费者的需求和商家的生产。【解析】这是一个含有同位语的复合句，同位语 process 后面带有一个定语从句。

64.【译文】但是，如果另一方面，增加产品产量能降低成本，那样生产商就会提高生产量，这样反过来会使产品价格下降，使得更多的消费者购买产品。【解析】这是一个含有条件状语从句的复合句，主句中还含有一个which引导的非限制性定语从句。

65.【译文】私人企业中一个重要的因素是个体可以拥有私有财产，可以雇用劳动力，获得自然资源，可以生产产品，提供服务，并能因此获得利润。【解析】这是一个含有两个并列的表语从句的复合句，分别由 that 引导。

##### Section IV Writing

##### 解题步骤：

第一步：审题

1. 题型：图示作文

2. 文体：说明文

第二步：框架分析

1. 一般文章格式

2. 点出每个要点

第三步：列出写作思路

1. 说明图表的含义

2. 阐明原因

3. 总结全文

第四步：列出关键词和词组

as can be seen in the table; great changes in people's diet in the past five years; used to be the Chinese main food; playing a less important role in people's diet; can be accounted for by a number of factors; brings the people opportunities to earn a lot of money; can afford to buy food that they want to eat; pay more attention to the structure of their diet; seeking the most reasonable diet structure; produced in large quantities; there will be greater changes in the future, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

As can be seen in the table, there have been great changes in people.'s diet in the past five years. Grain used to be the Chinese main food, but now it is playing a less important role in people's diet, while the consumption of some high energy foods, such as milk and meat, has increased steadily.

What caused these changes? The changes in diet can be accounted for by a number of factors. I think the reasons are as follows: First, the open policy brings the people opportunities to earn a lot of money. People are much wealthier than before. With a higher income, they can afford to buy food that they want to eat. Second, people nowadays pay more attention to the structure of their diet. They are seeking the most reasonable diet structure, which will do good to their health. Finally, owing to the economic reform, meat and milk, which were scare in the past, are produced in large quantities.

To sum up, with the development of the country, the level of the Chinese people's life has changed in the past five years. I'm sure there will be greater changes in the future.

as can be seen in the table; great changes in people's diet in the past five years; used to be the Chinese main food; playing a less important role in people's diet; can be accounted for by a number of factors; brings the people opportunities to earn a lot of money; can afford to buy food that they want to eat; pay more attention to the structure of their diet; seeking the most reasonable diet structure; produced in large quantities; there will be greater changes in the future, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

As can be seen in the table, there have been great changes in people.'s diet in the past five years. Grain used to be the Chinese main food, but now it is playing a less important role in people's diet, while the consumption of some high energy foods, such as milk and meat, has increased steadily.

What caused these changes? The changes in diet can be accounted for by a number of factors. #### 专家预测试题(一)

#### Section I Listening Comprehension

1. America 【解析】细节题。根据“The Library of Congress is America’s national library.”可知正确答案。

2. millions 【解析】细节题。根据“It has millions of books and other objects.”可知正确答案。

3. 1800 【解析】细节题。根据“The Library of Congress was established in 1800.”可知正确答案。

4. Britain 【解析】细节题。根据“They were all destroyed that year, when the Capitol was burned down during America's war with Britain.” 可知正确答案。

5. 1897 【解析】细节题。根据 “In 1897, the library moved into its own building across the street from the Capitol.” 可知正确答案。

6. New England towns 【解析】细节题。根据“County fairs are a tradition in New England towns.”可知正确答案。

7. empty of food 【解析】细节题。根据“make sure your stomach is nearly empty of food.” 可知正确答案。

8. right 【解析】细节题。根据“Placing your hands in the right position adds to the chances of winning.” 可知正确答案。

9. the middle 【解析】细节题。根据“It is usually better to start at the outside and work toward the middle.”可知正确答案。

10. cheers 【解析】细节题。根据“Let the cheers from the crowd spur you on.”可知正确答案。

11. A 【解析】细节题。根据文章第一句可知它可以降低死亡率。所以正确答案为 A。

12. D 【解析】细节题。文章讲了通过防止血变黏稠和血管堵塞可以降低生命危险。可知本题答案为D。

13. D 【解析】细节题。根据“... who have stomach bleeding or other bad reactions from aspirin should not take it after heart surgery.”故本题答案为D。

14. C 【解析】细节题。根据“I’m packed, but not quite ready.”可得出正确答案为C。

15. B 【解析】细节题。根据“It’s never cold in Rome”, 可知旅游目的地是罗马。因此正确答案为 B。

16. A 【解析】细节题。根据“I asked the taxi driver to wait ten minutes, not twenty.”可知正确答案为A。

17. A 【解析】细节题。根据“… everything was legal… in his pockets.”可知正确答案为A。

18. C 【解析】细节题。根据“Peter was an auto mechanic.”可知本题答案为C。

19. D 【解析】细节题。因为这是一场比赛，所以他们都在旁边观看。因此本题答案为D。

20. A 【解析】细节题。根据“When his time was up...”可知是由于没有时间了，所以本题选C。

#### 专家预测试题(一)

#### Section I Listening Comprehension

##### Part A

1. America 【解析】细节题。根据“The Library of Congress is America’s national library.”可知正确答案。

2. millions 【解析】细节题。根据“It has millions of books and other objects.”可知正确答案。

3. 1800 【解析】细节题。根据“The Library of Congress was established in 1800.”可知正确答案。

4. Britain 【解析】细节题。根据“They were all destroyed that year, when the Capitol was burned down during America's war with Britain.” 可知正确答案。

5. 1897 【解析】细节题。根据 “In 1897, the library moved into its own building across the street from the Capitol.” 可知正确答案。

##### Part B

6. New England towns 【解析】细节题。根据“County fairs are a tradition in New England towns.” 可知正确答案。

7. empty of food 【解析】细节题。根据“make sure your stomach is nearly empty of food.” 可知正确答案。

8. right 【解析】细节题。根据“Placing your hands in the right position adds to the chances of winning.” 可知正确答案。

9. the middle 【解析】细节题。根据“It is usually better to start at the outside and work toward the middle.”可知正确答案。

10. cheers 【解析】细节题。根据“Let the cheers from the crowd spur you on.”可知正确答案。

##### Part C

11. A 【解析】细节题。根据文章第一句可知它可以降低死亡率。所以正确答案为 A。

12. D 【解析】细节题。文章讲了通过防止血变黏稠和血管堵塞可以降低生命危险。可知本题答案为D。

13. D 【解析】细节题。根据“... who have stomach bleeding or other bad reactions from aspirin should not take it after heart surgery.”故本题答案为D。

14. C 【解析】细节题。根据“I’m packed, but not quite ready.”可得出正确答案为C。

15. B 【解析】细节题。根据“It’s never cold in Rome”, 可知旅游目的地是罗马。因此正确答案为B。

16. A 【解析】细节题。根据“I asked the taxi driver to wait ten minutes, not twenty.”可知正确答案为A。

17. A 【解析】细节题。根据“… everything was legal… in his pockets.”可知正确答案为A。

18. C 【解析】细节题。根据“Peter was an auto mechanic.”可知本题答案为C。

19. D 【解析】细节题。因为这是一场比赛，所以他们都在旁边观看。因此本题答案为D。

20. A 【解析】细节题。根据“When his time was up...”可知是由于没有时间了，所以本题选C。

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

The Library of Congress is America's national library. It has millions of books and other objects. It has newspapers and popular publications, as well as letters of historical interest. It also has maps,

photographs, art prints, movies, sound recordings, and musical instruments. All together, it has more than 100 million objects. The Library of Congress is open to the public Monday through Saturday except for public holidays. Anyone may go there and read anything in the collection. But no one is permitted to take books out of the building. The Library of Congress was established in 1800. It started with 11 boxes of books in one room of the Capitol Building. By 1814, the collection had increased to about 3,000 books. They were all destroyed that year, when the Capitol was burned down during America's war with Britain. To help rebuild the library, Congress bought the books of president Thomas Jefferson. Mr. Jefferson's collection included 7,000 books in seven languages. In 1897, the library moved into its own building across the street from the Capitol. Today, three buildings hold the library's collection. The library provides books and materials to the US Congress and also lends books to other American libraries, government agencies, and foreign libraries. It buys some of its books and gets others as gifts. It also gets materials through its copyright office. Anyone who wants copyright protection for a publication in the US must send two copies to the library. This means the Library of Congress receives almost everything that is published in the United States.

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

County fairs are a tradition in New England towns. They offer great entertainment. One popular event is the pie-eating contest. If you want to take part in the contest, it is a good idea to remember these guidelines: first, make sure your stomach is nearly empty of food. Eating a whole pie can be hard if you have just finish a meal. Next, it is helpful to like the pie you are going to eat. The cream types are a good choice. They slide down the throat more easily. Placing your hands in the right position adds to the chances of winning. There is a temptation to reach out and help the eating process. This will result in becoming disqualified. Don't just sit on your hands, if your hands are tied behind your back, you will not be tempted to make use of them.

Now you are ready to show your talent at eating pies. The object of course, is to get the bottom of the pie plate before the other people. It is usually better to start at the outside and work toward the middle. This method gives you a goal to focus on. Try not to notice what the other people near you are doing. Let the cheers from the crowd spur you on. But don't look up. All you should think about is eating that pie.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

A new study reports the common drug aspirin greatly reduces life threatening problems after an operation to replace blocked blood vessels to the heart. More than 800,000 people around the world have this heart surgery each year. The doctors who carried out the study say giving aspirin to patients soon after the operation could save thousands of lives. People usually take aspirin to control pain and reduce high body temperature. Doctors also advise some people to take aspirin to help prevent heart attacks. About 10—15 percent of these heart operations end in death or damage to the heart or other organs. The new study shows that even a small amount of aspirin reduced such threats. The doctors said the chance of death for patients who took aspirin would fall by 67%. They claimed this was true if the aspirin was given within 48 hours of the operation. The doctors believe aspirin helps heart surgery patients because it can prevent blood from thickening and blood vessels from being blocked. However, the doctors warned that people who have stomach bleeding or other bad reactions from aspirin should not take it after heart surgery.

听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

M: Mary, I hope you are packed and ready to leave.

W: Yes, I'm packed, but not quite ready. I can't find my passport.

M: Passport? That's the one thing you mustn't leave behind.

W: I know. I haven't lost it. I've packed it, but I can't remember which bag it's in.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

A new study reports the common drug aspirin greatly reduces life threatening problems after an operation to replace blocked blood vessels to the heart. More than 800,000 people around the world have this heart surgery each year. 听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

M: Mary, I hope you are packed and ready to leave.

W: Yes, I'm packed, but not quite ready. I can't find my passport.

M: Passport? That's the one thing you mustn't leave behind.

W: I know. I haven't lost it. I've packed it, but I can't remember which bag it's in.

M: Well, you have to find it at the airport. Come on, the taxi is waiting.

W: Did you say taxi? I thought we were going in your car.

M: Yes, well, I had planned to, but I'll explain later. You've got to be there in an hour.

W: The plane doesn't leave for two hours. Anyway, I'm ready to go now.

M: Now you are taking just one case, is that right?

W: No, there is one in the hall as well.

M: Gosh, what a lot of stuff! You're taking enough for a month instead of a week.

W: Well, you can't depend on the weather. It might be cold.

M: It's never cold in Rome. Certainly not in May. Come on, you really must go.

W: Right, we are ready. We've got the bags. I'm sure there is no need to rush.

M: There is. I asked the taxi driver to wait two minutes, not twenty.

W: Look, I'm supposed to be going away to relax. You're making me nervous.

M: Well, I want you to relax on holidays, but you can't relax yet.

W: OK, I promise not to relax, at least not until we get to the airport and I find my passport.

听第5段材料，回答第17至20题。

Some people dream of being President of the United States. Some dream of becoming stars in a Hollywood movie, and others of making millions of dollars overnight. But, could a dream like that come true in real life? Well, it did happen to Peter Johnson.

Peter was an auto mechanic. One day, he walked into the Union Trust Bank in Baltimore and took 5,000 dollars that did not belong to him. The guards and other employees stood back and let him stuff the bills in his shirt and pants without trying to prevent him from taking the money. No one tripped an alarm. No one pulled a gun. No one called the police. Why did they allow him to get away with it?

Well, everything was legal. Peter had won a contest promoted by a Baltimore radio station. The first prize entitled him to enter the Union Trust Bank and gather up as much money as he could lay his hands within five minutes. Because he could not bring any large bags or boxes into the bank, all the money had to be placed in his pockets.

As the time went by, Peter ran about wildly, trying to pick up as many large bills as he could find. When his time was up, he was out of breath, but was $5,000 richer.

##### Section II Use of English

[参考译文] 一个人的个人特征在一个人的智力发展过程中起着至关重要的作用。但是人们没有意识到在年轻人中培养这些因素的重要性。

所谓的非智力因素包括一个人的感情、意志、动机、兴趣和习惯。在追踪调查了8,000名男性之后，美国心理学家发现智力缺陷的主要原因不是智力本身，而是一些非智力因素，其中包括学习的欲望、意志力和自信心。

尽管人们都知道人应该有确定的目标、坚强的意志和良好的学习习惯，可是很多教师和家长都不太注重培养这些因素。

当孩子学习不好的时候一些家长很担心。他们责怪遗传因素、营养不良或是懒惰，但他们从未考虑过非智力因素。同时，一些老师也不探究诸如为什么学生们表现不佳这些问题。他们只是给他们更多的课程和练习，甚至批评讽刺他们。最终，这些学生会失去自信。一些学生感到很受挫，并绝望地放弃他们自己。一些学生可能由于厌学而误入歧途。一项对上海1,000多名中学生的调查显示，由于考试46.5%的学生害怕学习，36.4%的学生缺乏意志力、创造性和勤勉，10.3%的学生厌学

很明显，缺乏非智力因素的培养是青少年智力发展的主要障碍，甚至导致了一些学生身心发展的不平衡。

如果我们不从现在开始加强非智力因素的培养，将不仅阻碍青少年的智力发展，还会影响整个一代人的素质。一些专家已经提出了如何培养学生非智力因素的建议。

听第 5 段材料，回答第 17 至 20 题。

Some people dream of being President of the United States. Some dream of becoming stars in a Hollywood movie, and others of making millions of dollars overnight. But, could a dream like that come true in real life? Well, it did happen to Peter Johnson.

Peter was an auto mechanic. One day, he walked into the Union Trust Bank in Baltimore and took 5,000 dollars that did not belong to him. The guards and other employees stood back and let him stuff the bills in his shirt and pants without trying to prevent him from taking the money. No one tripped an alarm. No one pulled a gun. No one called the police. Why did they allow him to get away with it?

Well, everything was legal. Peter had won a contest promoted by a Baltimore radio station. The first prize entitled him to enter the Union Trust Bank and gather up as much money as he could lay his hands within five minutes. Because he could not bring any large bags or boxes into the bank, all the money had to be placed in his pockets.

As the time went by, Peter ran about wildly, trying to pick up as many large bills as he could find. When his time was up, he was out of breath, but was $5,000 richer.

##### Section II Use of English

[参考译文] 一个人的个人特征在一个人的智力发展过程中起着至关重要的作用。但是人们没有意识到在年轻人中培养这些因素的重要性。

所谓的非智力因素包括一个人的感情、意志、动机、兴趣和习惯。在追踪调查了8,000名男性之后，美国心理学家发现智力缺陷的主要原因不是智力本身，而是一些非智力因素，其中包括学习的欲望、意志力和自信心。

尽管人们都知道人应该有确定的目标、坚强的意志和良好的学习习惯，可是很多教师和家长都不太注重培养这些因素。

当孩子学习不好的时候一些家长很担心。他们责怪遗传因素、营养不良或是懒惰，但他们从未考虑过非智力因素。同时，一些老师也不探究诸如为什么学生们表现不佳这些问题。他们只是给他们更多的课程和练习，甚至批评讽刺他们。最终，这些学生会失去自信。一些学生感到很受挫，并绝望地放弃他们自己。一些学生可能由于厌学而误入歧途。一项对上海1,000多名中学生的调查显示，由于考试46.5%的学生害怕学习，36.4%的学生缺乏意志力、创造性和勤勉10.3%的学生厌学

很明显，缺乏非智力因素的培养是青少年智力发展的主要障碍，甚至导致了一些学生身心发展的不平衡。

如果我们不从现在开始加强非智力因素的培养，将不仅阻碍青少年的智力发展，还会影响整个一代人的素质。一些专家已经提出了如何培养学生非智力因素的建议。

首先，家长和老师应该完全了解青少年的心理。在此基础之上，他们就能帮学生追求学习的目标，刺激他们的兴趣，坚定他们的意志力。

21. A 【解析】本题考查代词。这里泛指人类，而不是特指某个人。故应选 A。

22. B 【解析】本题考查短语辨析。came out, 出来；found out, 发现；figured out, 弄清楚；worked out, 制定出。根据句意应选 B。

23. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。这里讲的是智力本身。in itself，在它本身；by itself，通过它自己；itself，它自己，本身；on its own，独立自主地。所以应选 C。

24. A 【解析】本题考查连词。根据句意应该是让步关系，故应选 A。

25. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。句意是培养这些能力，beliving, 相信；studying, 学习；cultivating, 培养；developing, 发展。所以应选 C。

26. B 【解析】本题考查连词。根据句意，这里应该是说当遇到这些问题的时候父母很担心。所以应选 B。

27. C 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。考虑到的英文表达是“take into consideration”。故正确答案为 C。

28. A 【解析】本题考查语法。…的原因英文表达是“reason for; reason that”, 故应选 A。

29. B 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据句意，老师们不但忽略这些因素，还有更不恰当的表现，程度上表示更进一步。所以应选 B。

30. D 【解析】本题考查动词短语。根据句意此处是指放弃自己，故用 give up。get up，起床；put up，搭起；handle 不能与 up 搭配。所以应选 D。

31. B 【解析】本题考查冠词。这里是泛指上海的一项调查，要用不定冠词。所以应选 B。

32. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据句意，此处是指由于考试而害怕学习。所以应选 A。

33. D 【解析】本题考查语法。这是一个 it 作形式主语的句子，真正的主语是一个从句，要用 that 引导。所以应选 D。

34. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。storage, 储藏；tendency, 趋势；threat, 威胁；obstacle, 障碍。根据句意应选 C。

35. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。intelligent，聪明的；characteristic，有特征的；psychological，心理的；physical 身体的，这里指的身心。所以应选 C。

36. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。chase,追逐；thrust,闯入；strengthen,加强；physical,身体的。根据句意应选C。

37. A 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。因为文章讲的是非智力因素和智力之间的关系。所以应选A。

38. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。projects, 工程；warnings, 警告；proposals, 建议；decision, 决定。句子讲的是提出建议，所以应选 C。

39. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据常识要想帮助他们就应该完全了解他们。所以应选 A。

40. D 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。insuring, 确保；going, 去；exceeding, 超过；stimulating, 刺激。可见，只有 D 符合文意。

##### Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Text 1

【文章概述】本文讲到了高温天气会给人体带来危害，并介绍了一些防热降温的方法。

41. D 【解析】细节题。由第一段“Floods, storms and other natural events kill thousands of people every year. So does extreme heat.”可知前三项都是主要原因。所以应选D。

42. A 【解析】判断题。根据“The most common health problem linked to hot weather is heat stress”可知 C 正确，其他选项都与文章的论述相反。所以应选 A。

24. A 【解析】本题考查连词。根据句意应该是让步关系，故应选 A。

25. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。句意是培养这些能力，beliving, 相信；studying, 学习；cultivating, 培养；developing, 发展。所以应选 C。

26. B 【解析】本题考查连词。根据句意，这里应该是说当遇到这些问题的时候父母很担心。所以应选 B。

27. C 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。考虑到的英文表达是“take into consideration”。故正确答案为 C。

28. A 【解析】本题考查语法。…的原因英文表达是“reason for; reason that”, 故应选 A。

29. B 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据句意，老师们不但忽略这些因素，还有更不恰当的表现，程度上表示更进一步。所以应选 B。

30. D 【解析】本题考查动词短语。根据句意此处是指放弃自己，故用 give up。get up，起床；put up，搭起；handle 不能与 up 搭配。所以应选 D。

31. B 【解析】本题考查冠词。这里是泛指上海的一项调查，要用不定冠词。所以应选 B。

32. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据句意，此处是指由于考试而害怕学习。所以应选 A。

33. D 【解析】本题考查语法。这是一个 it 作形式主语的句子，真正的主语是一个从句，要用 that 引导。所以应选 D。

34. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。storage, 储藏；tendency, 趋势；threat, 威胁；obstacle, 障碍。根据句意应选 C。

35. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。intelligent，聪明的；characteristic，有特征的；psychological，心理的；physical 身体的，这里指的身心。所以应选 C。

36. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。chase,追逐；thrust,闯入；strengthen,加强；physical,身体的。根据句意应选 C。

37. A 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。因为文章讲的是非智力因素和智力之间的关系。所以应选A。

38. C 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。projects, 工程；warnings, 警告；proposals, 建议；decision, 决定。句子讲的是提出建议，所以应选 C。

39. A 【解析】本题考查句意理解。根据常识要想帮助他们就应该完全了解他们。所以应选 A。

40. D 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。insuring, 确保；going, 去；exceeding, 超过；stimulating, 刺激。可见，只有 D 符合文意。

##### Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Part A

##### Text 1

【文章概述】本文讲到了高温天气会给人体带来危害，并介绍了一些防热降温的方法。

41. D 【解析】细节题。由第一段“Floods, storms and other natural events kill thousands of people every year. So does extreme heat.”可知前三项都是主要原因。所以应选D。

42. A 【解析】判断题。根据“The most common health problem linked to hot weather is heat stress”可知 C 正确，其他选项都与文章的论述相反。所以应选 A。

43. A,【解析】细节题。根据“The pain is a warning that the body is becoming too hot.”可知这是身体太热的警告。所以应选A。

44. D 【解析】细节题。文章第八段讲的是专家的建议。A、B、C三项都是专家建议要尽量避免的行为，只有D是提倡的行为。所以应选D。

45. A 【解析】主旨题。文章讲的都是高热的情况以及如何预防。所以应选 A。

##### Text 2

[文章概述] 本文对 Rhett Butler 致力于建立、扩展 Mongabay.com——一个关于雨林和雨林的存在面临的威胁的网站——的努力及所取得的成果做了介绍。

46. C 【解析】细节题。由第一段第三句可知他建立了网站。所以应选 C。

47. D 【解析】推断题。由“Mongabay has grown and led to other sites.”可推理出这个网站应该是发展了所以才吸引了别人，故 B 与原文相符。A 项与第三段第二句意思一致。C 是原文的细节。所以应选 D。

48. A 【解析】语义题。这一段讲的都是关于动物的事情，所以可以推测出 biologist 是指研究动物的专家。所以应选 A。

49. D 【解析】细节题。根据第六段中的“In April, Time.com named it one of the fifteen top climate and environment Web sites.” 可知它主要是关于气候和环境的网站。所以应选 D。

50. C 【解析】细节题。根据最后一段中的“he says the falling price of oil could reduce interest in developing solar power.”可知影响的是环保。所以应选 C。

##### Text 3

[文章概述] 本文讲述了作者在老挝的一次令她感动的购物经历。

51. A 【解析】判断题。B、C、D 在原文都可以找到，只有 A 文章并没有明确提到。所以应选 A。

52. C 【解析】细节题。根据“She was so pleased that unexpectedly, she accepted the last offer I made.” 可知是由于她很高兴。所以应选 C。

53. A 【解析】细节题。文章第五段给出了原因——是由于价格便宜。所以应选 A。

54. C 【解析】细节题。根据“I have learned to defend myself against what is hard...”可知是由于她学会了如何应对困难，所以应选C。

55. C 【解析】推断题。文章最后一段第一句说在慷慨的灵魂面前没有什么可以防备。也就是说作者被妇女的慷慨感动了，这种感动使她哭了。所以应选 C。

##### Text 4

[文章概述] 本文作者主要讲了家人的情况——由最初的没梦想到追求伟大的理想的过程。

56. C 【解析】细节题。由第一段第一句话 “My father was, I am sure, intended by nature to be a cheerful kindly man.” 可知父亲天生就是一个快活、和善的人。所以应选 C。

57. A 【解析】语义题。take possession of, 占据…, 所以应该是这种想法充斥了他们的大脑。所以应选 A。

58. D 【解析】细节题。文章最后一段主要介绍了母亲，是由于她，父亲才走上了追求梦想的道路，也就是母亲具有伟大的理想。所以应选D。

59. B 【解析】细节题。由第四段中的“… as I lay beside her—in the days of her—lying-in—she may have dreamed that I would someday rule men and cities.”可知她想让作者成为出色的政治家。所以应选 B。

60. C 【解析】主旨题。文章主要讲了家人的情况——由最初的没梦想到追求伟大理想的过程，并没有单纯介绍某一个人，所以应选 C。

[文章概述] 本文作者主要讲了家人的情况——由最初的没梦想到追求伟大的理想的过程。

60. C 【解析】主旨题。文章主要讲了家人的情况——由最初的没梦想到追求伟大理想的过程，并没有单纯介绍某一个人，所以应选 C。

61.【译文】科学研究的方法不过是人类思维活动的必要表达方式，也就是对一切现象进行思索并给以精确而严谨的解释的表达方式。【解析】这是一个由分号连接的并列句，其中第二个分句中含有一个由“介词+关系词”引导的定语从句修饰 mode。而且 nothing but 意为“仅仅，只”。

62.【译文】你们已经听说过，科学家是用归纳法和演绎法工作的，他们用这些方法，在某种意义上说，力求从自然界找出某些自然规律，然后他们根据这些规律，用自己的某种非同一般的本领，建立起他们的理论。【解析】这是一个含有三个宾语从句的复合句，都由 that 引导。

63.【译文】许多人以为，普通人的思维活动根本无法与科学家的思维过程相比，并认为这些思维过程必须经过某种专门训练才能掌握。【解析】这是一个it作形式主语的句子，其中真正的主语是两个that引导的主语从句。

64.【译文】在莫里哀的一个剧本中有这样一个著名的插曲：作者让主人公在得知他整个一生中一直在说散文后，表现出无限的喜悦。【解析】这是一个含有非限制性定语从句的复合句由where 引导而且其中还有一个由 that 引导的宾语从句。

65.【译文】同样，我认为，当你一旦发现你在一生中一直在按归纳法和演绎法的哲理办事时，你也会感到欣慰和陶醉。【解析】这是一个由that引导的含两个从句的复合句。第一个是宾语从句，第二个是同位语从句。

##### Section IV Writing

##### 解题步骤：

第一步：审题

1. 题型：图示作文

2. 文体：说明文

第二步：框架分析

1. 普通文章格式

2. 点出每个要点

第三步：列出写作思路

1. 说明图的内容

2. 产生变化的原因

第四步：列出关键词和词组

with the appearance of the Internet; a good indicator of this new lifestyle; boosted by the wide spread of the Internet; has both advantages and disadvantages; save money; relieve traffic congestion and save energy for society; everything has two sides; no exception; aims the boundary between professional and personal life; do private things during working hours; become more and more popular, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

With the appearance of the Internet, a new lifestyle — Soho, namely, small or home office, is developing quickly in the world. The increasing numbers of Sohoers in the graph is a good indicator of this new lifestyle. Boosted by the wide spread of the Internet, this new lifestyle is growing very fast. We can see this tendency in the graph. It is growing steadily since it appears.

I think staying home on the job has both advantages and disadvantages. Working at home with a computer, one needn't take a bus or ride to and from work. This can save money and time for Sohoers but also relieve traffic congestion and save energy for society. It is also a paradise for those who don't like to be confined by all the regulations at the company.

But everything has two sides. Soho is no exception. The home environment aims at the boundary between professional and personal life. Some Sohoers are sometimes tempted to do private things during working hours. In addition, the impersonal nature of communication in cyberspace sometimes depresses Sohoers.

##### Section IV Writing

1. 题型：图示作文

2. 文体：说明文

2. 点出每个要点

第四步：列出关键词和词组

with the appearance of the Internet; a good indicator of this new lifestyle; boosted by the wide spread of the Internet; has both advantages and disadvantages; save money; relieve traffic congestion and save energy for society; everything has two sides; no exception; aims the boundary between professional and personal life; do private things during working hours; become more and more popular, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

With the appearance of the Internet, a new lifestyle — Soho, namely, small or home office, is developing quickly in the world. The increasing numbers of Sohoers in the graph is a good indicator of this new lifestyle. Boosted by the wide spread of the Internet, this new lifestyle is growing very fast. We can see this tendency in the graph. It is growing steadily since it appears.

I think staying home on the job has both advantages and disadvantages. Working at home with a computer, one needn't take a bus or ride to and from work. This can save money and time for Sohoers but also relieve traffic congestion and save energy for society. It is also a paradise for those who don't like to be confined by all the regulations at the company.

But everything has two sides. Soho is no exception. The home environment aims at the boundary between professional and personal life. Some Sohoers are sometimes tempted to do private things during working hours. In addition, the impersonal nature of communication in cyberspace sometimes depresses Sohoers.

After all, it's a new lifestyle. I believe with the development of society it will become more and more popular.

# 专家预测试题（二）

##### Section I Listening Comprehension

1. Japan 【解析】细节题。根据“Most of these are made in Japan.”可知正确答案。

2. auto-focus 【解析】细节题。根据“…our researchers recommend the Olympic BY model as the best auto-focus camera available at the moment.”可知正确答案。

3. $200 【解析】细节题。根据 “It costs 200 dollars although you may well want to spend more...” 可知正确答案。

4. American 【解析】细节题。根据 “the American versions are considerably more expensive...” 可知正确答案。

5.320 【解析】细节题。根据“… The Olympic BY model weighs only 320 grams…”可知正确答案。

6. commit suicide 【解析】细节题。根据“Someone is threatening to commit suicide.”可知正确答案。

7. despair and misery 【解析】细节题。根据 “… would-be jumpers are driven to despair and see no hope out of their misery.” 可知正确答案。

8. channels of communication 【解析】细节题。根据 “The trick is to open channels of communication with them.” 可知正确答案。

9. about thirty 【解析】细节题。根据 “Larry estimates he has rescued about thirty people in his ten years of service.” 可知正确答案。

10. ten years 【解析】细节题。根据“Larry estimates he has rescued about thirty people in his ten years of service.” 可知正确答案。

11. A 【解析】细节题。上文说第一个孩子可能成功是由于父母给了他们太多的关注，下文说独生子女成功的原因也一样。由此可知本题答案为 A。

12. C 【解析】推断题。文中说中间的孩子在家里像被丢失了一样，也就是说他们经常被人忽略，故本题答案为 C。

13. C 【解析】细节题。文中说他们喜欢按照自己的想法做事情。可知本题答案为 C。

14. D 【解析】推断题。文中说残疾人容易成为受害者，盲人也属于残疾人，由此可推断出正确答案为D。

15. A 【解析】细节题。文章建议将钱包放在紧身裤的后兜里，因此正确答案为 A。

16. B 【解析】细节题。文章指出服装店是行窃最好的地方，因为人们忙着挑衣服。因此可知正确答案为 B。

17. C 【解析】细节题。根据“It was about a small animal…”可知正确答案为C。

18. A 【解析】细节题。根据“… support myself by writing.”可知本题答案为 A。

19. D 【解析】细节题。根据“The ideas come first, so it really depends on the idea that grabs me next!” 可知她是根据自己的想法。因此本题答案为 D。

20. B 【解析】细节题。根据“I’ve no idea where ideas come from…”可知本题选 B。

## 134
