After all, it's a new lifestyle. I believe with the development of society it will become more and more popular.

#### 专家预测试题（二）

##### Section I Listening Comprehension

##### Part A

1. Japan 【解析】细节题。根据“Most of these are made in Japan.”可知正确答案。

2. auto-focus 【解析】细节题。根据“…our researchers recommend the Olympic BY model as the best auto-focus camera available at the moment.”可知正确答案。

3. $200 【解析】细节题。根据 “It costs 200 dollars although you may well want to spend more...” 可知正确答案。

4. American 【解析】细节题。根据 “the American versions are considerably more expensive...” 可知正确答案。

5.320 【解析】细节题。根据“… The Olympic BY model weighs only 320 grams…”可知正确答案。

##### Part B

6. commit suicide 【解析】细节题。根据“Someone is threatening to commit suicide.”可知正确答案。

7. despair and misery 【解析】细节题。根据 “… would-be jumpers are driven to despair and see no hope out of their misery.” 可知正确答案。

8. channels of communication 【解析】细节题。根据 “The trick is to open channels of communication with them.” 可知正确答案。

9. about thirty 【解析】细节题。根据 “Larry estimates he has rescued about thirty people in his ten years of service.” 可知正确答案。

10. ten years 【解析】细节题。根据“Larry estimates he has rescued about thirty people in his ten years of service.” 可知正确答案。

##### Part C

11. A 【解析】细节题。上文说第一个孩子可能成功是由于父母给了他们太多的关注，卜文说独生子女成功的原因也一样。由此可知本题答案为 A。

12. C 【解析】推断题。文中说中间的孩子在家里像被丢失了一样，也就是说他们经常被人忽略，故本题答案为 C。

13. C 【解析】细节题。文中说他们喜欢按照自己的想法做事情。可知本题答案为 C。

14. D 【解析】推断题。文中说残疾人容易成为受害者，盲人也属于残疾人，由此可推断出正确答案为D。

15. A 【解析】细节题。文章建议将钱包放在紧身裤的后兜里，因此正确答案为 A。

16. B 【解析】细节题。文章指出服装店是行窃最好的地方，因为人们忙着挑衣服。因此可知正确答案为 B。

17. C 【解析】细节题。根据“It was about a small animal…”可知正确答案为C。

18. A 【解析】细节题。根据“… support myself by writing.”可知本题答案为 A。

19. D 【解析】细节题。根据“The ideas come first, so it really depends on the idea that grabs me next!” 可知她是根据自己的想法。因此本题答案为 D。

20. B 【解析】细节题。根据“I’ve no idea where ideas come from…”可知本题选 B。

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

There are a lot of good cameras available at the moment. Most of these are made in Japan. But there are also good quality models from Germany and the USA. We have investigated an arrangement of models to see which has the best value for money. After a number of different tests and interviews with people who are familiar with different cameras being accessed, our researchers recommend the Olympic BY model as the best auto-focus camera available at the moment. It costs 200 dollars although you may well want to spend more, perhaps as much as another 200 dollars on buying additional and other equipment. It is a good Japanese camera easy to use, equivalent to the other model's less expensive and slightly less easy to use, while the American versions are considerably more expensive. The Olympic BY model weighs only 320 grams, which is quite a bit less than other cameras of the similar type. Indeed, one of the other models we looked at weighs almost twice as much. Similarly, it's smaller than most of its competitors, thus fitting easily into a pocket or handbag. All the people we interviewed expressed almost total satisfaction with it. The only problem was slight awkwardness in loading the film.

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

Larry Smith is one of the rescuers on the Golden Gate Bridge. His job is to save people who attempt to jump into the sea. If the telephone rings at three in the morning, he knows it's trouble. Someone is threatening to commit suicide. Larry will get his things ready, and in no time he'll be out the door and heading to the spot.

“If you aren't too late,” Larry says, “you climb out onto the cold steel and try to talk to the poor, and pull him anyhow back to safety. For many suicide attempts are made on the spur of the moment, and lives can be saved. But if you fall, if the person jumps into the bottomless pool, there's no describing how helpless you feel. Often, would-be jumpers are driven to despair and see no hope out of their misery. The trick is to open channels of communication with them. Following are some tricks that have worked: If you're going to jump, at least give me your mom's phone number so I can call and tell her. Or ‘That's a nice watch. If you're going to jump, can I have it?’ Sometimes, all it takes is the voice of the human being who cares.”

Larry estimates he has rescued about thirty people in his ten years of service.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

Were you the first or the last child in your family? Or were you a middle or an only child? Some people think it matters where you were born in your family. But there are different ideas about what birth order means. Some people say that oldest children are smart and strong-willed. They are very likely to be successful. The reason for this is simple. Parents have a lot of time for their first child, and they give him or her a lot of attention. So this child is very likely to do well. An only child will succeed for the same reason. What happens to the other children in the family? Middle children don't get so much attention, so they don't feel that important. If a family has many children, the middle one sometimes gets lost in the crowd. The youngest child, though, often gets special treatment. He or she is the baby. Often this child grows up to be funny and charming. Do you believe these ideas of birth order too? A recent study saw things quite differently. The study found that first children believed in family rules. They didn't take many chances in their lives. They usually followed orders. Rules didn't mean as much to later children in the family. They went out and followed their own ideas. They took chances and they often did better in life.

听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

For 25 years I was a full-time thief, specializing in picking pockets. I come from southeast London, where that's an honorable profession. Anyone can break into a house and steal things. But picking somebody's pocket takes skill. My sister and I were among the most successful pickpocket teams in

The only problem was slight awkwardness in loading the film.

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

For many suicide attempts are made on the spur of the moment, and lives can be saved. But if you fall, if the person jumps into the bottomless pool, there's no describing how helpless you feel. Often, would-be jumpers are driven to despair and see no hope out of their misery. The trick is to open channels of communication with them. Following are some tricks that have worked: If you're going to jump, at least give me your mom's phone number so I can call and tell her. Or ‘That's a nice watch. If you're going to jump, can I have it?’ Sometimes, all it takes is the voice of the human being who cares.”

Larry estimates he has rescued about thirty people in his ten years of service.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

Were you the first or the last child in your family? Or were you a middle or an only child? Some people think it matters where you were born in your family. But there are different ideas about what birth order means. Some people say that oldest children are smart and strong-willed. They are very likely to be successful. The reason for this is simple. Parents have a lot of time for their first child, and they give him or her a lot of attention. So this child is very likely to do well. An only child will succeed for the same reason. What happens to the other children in the family? Middle children don't get so much attention, so they don't feel that important. If a family has many children, the middle one sometimes gets lost in the crowd. The youngest child, though, often gets special treatment. He or she is the baby. Often this child grows up to be funny and charming. Do you believe these ideas of birth order too? A recent study saw things quite differently. The study found that first children believed in family rules. They didn't take many chances in their lives. They usually followed orders. Rules didn't mean as much to later children in the family. They went out and followed their own ideas. They took chances and they often did better in life.

听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

For 25 years I was a full-time thief, specializing in picking pockets. I come from southeast London, where that's an honorable profession. Anyone can break into a house and steal things. But picking somebody's pocket takes skill. My sister and I were among the most successful pickpocket teams in

London. We worked in hotel and theatre lobbies, airports, shopping centers, restaurants. Now we don't steal anymore, but this crime is worldwide. Here is how to protect yourself.

Professional pickpockets do not see victims, only handbags, jewels and money. Mothers with babies, the elderly, and the disabled are all fair game. My preferred target was the lone female, handbag at her side, the right side to be exact. So if I'm next to her I can reach it cautiously with my right hand across my body. Only about one woman in a thousand carries her bag on the left, and I tended to steer clear of them. Women whose bags are hanging in front of them are tricky for the pickpocket, as there isn't a blind side. If you want to make it even harder, use a bag with handles rather than a strap. For men, one of the best places to keep a wallet is in the back pocket of tight trousers. You'll feel any attempts to move it. Another good place is in the buttoned-up inside pocket of a jacket. There's just no way in. Even better, keep wallets attached to a cord or chain that is fasten to a belt.

A pickpocket needs targets who are relaxed and off guard. The perfect setting is clothing store. When customs wander among the racks, they are completely absorbed in the items they hold up. The presence of a uniformed security guard is even better. A false sense of security makes a pickpocket's job much simpler.

听第5段材料，回答第17至20题。

M: Hi, Ms. Rowling. How old were you when you started to write, and what was your first book?

W: I wrote my first finished story when I was about 6. It was about a small animal, a rabbit I mean, and I've been writing ever since.

M: Why did you choose to be an author?

W: If someone asked me how to achieve happiness, step one would be finding out what you love doing most and step two would be finding someone to pay you to do it. I consider myself very lucky indeed to be able to support myself by writing.

M: Do you have any plans to write books for adults?

W: My first two novels were for adults. I suppose I might write another one, but I never really imagine a target audience when I'm writing. The ideas come first, so it really depends on the idea that grabs me next!

M: Where did the ideas for the Harry Potter books come from?

W: I've no idea where ideas come from and I hope I never find out. It would spoil my excitement if it turned out I just have a funny little wrinkle on the surface of my brain which makes me think about invisible train platforms.

M: How do you come up with the names of your characters?

W: I invented some of the names in the Harry books, but I also collect strange names. I've gotten them from medieval saints, maps, dictionaries, plants, war memorials, and people I've met!

#### Section II. Use of English

【参考译文】尽管很多政府试图说服各自的国民，原子能是化石燃料的一种可接受的替代品，但是没有政府付出一点努力来解释它的危害。他们可能没意识到，无论什么原因，公众必须通过经验了解，即使这种经历可能是灾难性的。

尽管核反应堆不会产生可见的烟雾，但它确实会污染环境。而且由于是看不见的，其产生的污染应该更严重。

不管政府公开事实多么的不便，他们也没有什么道德理由不这样做，即使他们是为我们的最大利益着想。至少有些事实是知道的，虽然没有被广泛公布。

核反应堆产生了大量的具有放射性的水和气体。这些废物怎么办呢？它们被放入水泥罐里，存放在油库中，储存在废弃的盐矿中；流入碎裂的岩石中；被掩埋，用特别的火车运输。但即使被掩埋时，也要喷水使他保持冷却，防止沸腾。当然，容器里的物质的腐蚀性也是非常强的。容器破裂或是

London. We worked in hotel and theatre lobbies, airports, shopping centers, restaurants. Now we don't steal anymore, but this crime is worldwide. Here is how to protect yourself.

Professional pickpockets do not see victims, only handbags, jewels and money. Mothers with babies, the elderly, and the disabled are all fair game. Only about one woman in a thousand carries her bag on the left, and I tended to steer clear of them. Women whose bags are hanging in front of them are tricky for the pickpocket, as there isn't a blind side. If you want to make it even harder, use a bag with handles rather than a strap. For men, one of the best places to keep a wallet is in the back pocket of tight trousers. You'll feel any attempts to move it. Another good place is in the buttoned-up inside pocket of a jacket. There's just no way in. Even better, keep wallets attached to a cord or chain that is fasten to a belt.

A pickpocket needs targets who are relaxed and off guard. The perfect setting is clothing store. When customs wander among the racks, they are completely absorbed in the items they hold up. The presence of a uniformed security guard is even better. A false sense of security makes a pickpocket's job much simpler.

听第5段材料，回答第17至20题。

M: Hi, Ms. Rowling. How old were you when you started to write, and what was your first book?

W: I wrote my first finished story when I was about 6. It was about a small animal, a rabbit I mean, and I've been writing ever since.

M: Why did you choose to be an author?

W: If someone asked me how to achieve happiness, step one would be finding out what you love doing most and step two would be finding someone to pay you to do it. I consider myself very lucky indeed to be able to support myself by writing.

M: Do you have any plans to write books for adults?

W: My first two novels were for adults. I suppose I might write another one, but I never really imagine a target audience when I'm writing. The ideas come first, so it really depends on the idea that grabs me next!

M: Where did the ideas for the Harry Potter books come from?

W: I've no idea where ideas come from and I hope I never find out. It would spoil my excitement if it turned out I just have a funny little wrinkle on the surface of my brain which makes me think about invisible train platforms.

M: How do you come up with the names of your characters?

W: I invented some of the names in the Harry books, but I also collect strange names. I've gotten them from medieval saints, maps, dictionaries, plants, war memorials, and people I've met!

#### Section II. Use of English

【参考译文】尽管很多政府试图说服各自的国民，原子能是化石燃料的一种可接受的替代品，但是没有政府付出一点努力来解释它的危害。他们可能没意识到，无论什么原因，公众必须通过经验了解，即使这种经历可能是灾难性的。

尽管核反应堆不会产生可见的烟雾，但它确实会污染环境。而且由于是看不见的，其产生的污染应该更严重。

不管政府公开事实多么的不便，他们也没有什么道德理由不这样做，即使他们是为我们的最大利益着想。至少有些事实是知道的，虽然没有被广泛公布。

核反应堆产生了大量的具有放射性的水和气体。这些废物怎么办呢？它们被放入水泥罐里，存放在油库中，储存在废弃的盐矿中；流入碎裂的岩石中；被掩埋，用特别的火车运输。但即使被掩埋时，也要喷水使他保持冷却，防止沸腾。当然，容器里的物质的腐蚀性也是非常强的。容器破裂或是

制冷系统失灵的后果都会是灾难性的。

虽然采取了各种措施确保这些放射性废物不扩散到海里或饮用水里，那种泄漏太可怕了，简直不敢想象。但即使那样，政府也很可能会继续把危害放小。

似乎政府不公布事实也不会受到任何指责，他们会继续保持沉默。尽管人民有权知道全部事实真相。你知道你们国家的放射性废弃物是怎么处理的吗？不知道？那就去查一查吧！

21. C 【解析】本题考查介词。某物的替代应该用介词 to。for，为了；with 带有；instead of 常用来否定后边的部分。故应选 C。

22. A 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。这是作者对政府这种不作为的一种解释，猜测他们是没有意识到其危害性。所以应选 A。

23. B 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。无论什么原因的英文表达是“whatever the reason”，有时后边会加上 may be。所以应选 B。

24. D 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。上文说公众只能通过经验了解，下文又退了一步，可以确定上下文是讲同一件东西。故应选D。

25. C 【解析】本题考查句意理解。此句是一个 while 引导的转折句根据上下文可知本句意为“尽管核反应堆不会产生可见的烟雾，但它确实会污染环境。”所以应选 C。

26. B 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。此处前半句说到它们造成的污染是隐伏的，可见它是“看不见的”，所以只能选 B。

27. A 【解析】本题考查连词。这里表示一种让步，指无论什么情况，故正确答案为 A。

28. D 【解析】本题考查连词。后边退一步讲了一种比较好的情况，应选一个表示“即使……”意义的词，故应选 D。

29. C 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。大量的英文表达是“in large quantities”。所以应选 C。

30. A 【解析】本题考查语法。某物发生某种情况的英文表达是“sth. happen to sb./sth.”，在这又是作谓语。所以应选 A。

31. B 【解析】本题考查动词短语辨析。put down, 镇压；put into, 放入，注入；put up, 搭起；put onto, 加入。所以应选 B。

32. A 【解析】本题考查语法。这是被动语态，而且通篇都是一般现在时。所以应选 A。

33. D 【解析】本题考查语法。本题考查的内容和上题一样。所以应选D。

34. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。后面讲到要洒水，由此可知应保持凉爽。所以应选 C。

35. D 【解析】本题考查语法。分析句子可知所填的成分在句子中作表语，应该用形容词。所以应选D。

36. B 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。reassure，安慰；ensure，确保；convince，说服；assure，保证。句意应该是讲要确保安全。所以应选 B。

37. D 【解析】本题考查介词。句意是讲应倒入水里。所以应选 D。

38. C 【解析】本题考查语法。本题考查的是 too... to... 结构，故应选 C。

39. A 【解析】本题考查语法。这里需要一个引导从句的词组所以只能选 A, 只要; B、C、D 都只能表示程度。

40. D 【解析】本题考查语法。这里需要谓语，故 B、C 不正确；而人民表示复数意义，所以谓语要用复数。所以应选 D。

##### Text 1

[文章概述] 本文讲述了音乐文化的发展与它的物质层面的发展之间的关系。

41. C 【解析】细节题。根据文章第一段第二句“Examining a culture’s tools and technology can tell

### 参考答案与解析

制冷系统失灵的后果都会是灾难性的。

虽然采取了各种措施确保这些放射性废物不扩散到海里或饮用水里，那种泄漏太可怕了，简直不敢想象。但即使那样，政府也很可能会继续把危害放小。

似乎政府不公布事实也不会受到任何指责，他们会继续保持沉默。尽管人民有权知道全部事实真相。你知道你们国家的放射性废弃物是怎么处理的吗？不知道？那就去查一查吧！

21. C 【解析】本题考查介词。某物的替代应该用介词 to。for，为了；with 带有；instead of 常用来否定后边的部分。故应选 C。

22. A 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。这是作者对政府这种不作为的一种解释，猜测他们是没有意识到其危害性。所以应选 A。

23. B 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。无论什么原因的英文表达是“whatever the reason”，有时后边会加上 may be。所以应选 B。

24. D 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。上文说公众只能通过经验了解，下文又退了一步，可以确定上下文是讲同一件东西。故应选D。

25. C 【解析】本题考查句意理解。此句是一个 while 引导的转折句根据上下文可知本句意为“尽管核反应堆不会产生可见的烟雾，但它确实会污染环境。”所以应选 C。

26. B 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。此处前半句说到它们造成的污染是隐伏的，可见它是“看不见的”，所以只能选 B。

27. A 【解析】本题考查连词。这里表示一种让步，指无论什么情况，故正确答案为 A。

28. D 【解析】本题考查连词。后边退一步讲了一种比较好的情况，应选一个表示“即使……”意义的词，故应选 D。

29. C 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。大量的英文表达是“in large quantities”。所以应选 C。

30. A 【解析】本题考查语法。某物发生某种情况的英文表达是“sth. happen to sb./sth.”，在这又是作谓语。所以应选 A。

31. B 【解析】本题考查动词短语辨析。put down, 镇压；put into, 放入，注入；put up, 搭起；put onto, 加入。所以应选 B。

32. A 【解析】本题考查语法。这是被动语态，而且通篇都是一般现在时。所以应选 A。

33. D 【解析】本题考查语法。本题考查的内容和上题一样。所以应选D。

34. C 【解析】本题考查上下文理解。后面讲到要洒水，由此可知应保持凉爽。所以应选 C。

35. D 【解析】本题考查语法。分析句子可知所填的成分在句子中作表语，应该用形容词。所以应选D。

36. B 【解析】本题考查词义辨析。reassure, 安慰；ensure, 确保；convince, 说服；assure, 保证。句意应该是讲要确保安全。所以应选 B。

37. D 【解析】本题考查介词。句意是讲应倒入水里。所以应选D。

38. C 【解析】本题考查语法。本题考查的是 too... to... 结构，故应选 C。

39. A 【解析】本题考查语法。这里需要一个引导从句的词组所以只能选 A, 只要; B、C、D 都只能表示程度。

40. D 【解析】本题考查语法。这里需要谓语，故 B、C 不正确；而人民表示复数意义，所以谓语要用复数。所以应选 D。

# Section III Reading Comprehension

Part A

##### Text 1

【文章概述】本文讲述了音乐文化的发展与它的物质层面的发展之间的关系。

41. C 【解析】细节题。根据文章第一段第二句 “Examining a culture’s tools and technology can tell

us about the nation's history and way of life."可知C选项符合原文和题目要求。A、B、D三项文中都未提及，故都不正确。所以应选C。

42. B 【解析】细节题。B 项与第一段最后半句话意思一致，符合原文和题目要求。其他三项与文章中的论述都不相符，故都不正确。所以应选 B。

43. A 【解析】细节题。A项符合文章第二段最后一句话的意思，且符合题目要求。其他三项都与原文和题目要求不符，故都不正确。所以应选A。

44. C 【解析】推断题。C符合原文以及题目要求。其他选项都与原文不符。故应选C。

45. D 【解析】主旨题。文章是讲音乐文化的发展相当依靠它的物质层面的发展，这与D项的意思一致。A、B、C三项都比较片面。所以应选D。

##### Text 2

[文章概述] 本文主要讲到宗谱学可以利用 DNA 来把个人与其家族联系在一起。

46. B 【解析】理解题。题干是对第一句中的“there are also regions of the DNA strand”的改写。前面提到“DNA 具有遗传性”，既然是遗传，那么可以推断出具有同样 DNA 特征的人必定有同样的祖先。所以应选 B。

47. C 【解析】语义题。基于第一段第二、三句，我们知道遗传标记提供独有的识别特征把人们连接在一起，形成家族树。换句话说，能对家族树进行研究，是因为遗传标记的独有特征可以用来判断一个人的基因身份以及这个人与他人之间的关系。所以正确答案为 C。

48. A 【解析】细节题。根据第二段中的“... help in solving many issues regarding genealogies that could not be done by relying only...”可知，建立数据库有助于解决仅靠其他方法不能解决的与宗谱有关的许多问题。所以应选 A。

49. B 【解析】细节题。根据文章最后一段可知通过采集两个人的 DNA 样本我们可以证实两个人是否有同样的祖先。因此正确答案为 B。

50. C 【解析】推断题。文章说他们与家族树的关系是基因特征而不是其他因素决定的，可见 C 与原文不符。故应选 C。

##### Text 3

[文章概述] 本文讲述了 Glenn Kreamer 面临火灾时，沉着冷静救助母亲的故事。

51. C 【解析】细节题。文章第二段告诉我们他跑到楼下是为了救哥哥和姐姐。所以应选 C。

52. D 【解析】细节题。文章倒数第二段具体讲了他救母亲的办法。所以应选D。

53. B 【解析】细节题。文章讲了他打电话给父亲，让父亲给消防队和医院打电话。所以应选 B。

54. A 【解析】细节题。文章第五段告诉我们他是头上蒙着一块湿布跑出去的。所以应选 A。

55. C 【解析】理解题。文章并没有讲谁教他或是指导他救人，而完全是靠自己的机警和沉着。所以应选 C。

##### Text 4

[文章概述] 本文主要讲为了保证旅行计划顺利实施，旅行之前需要做的一些准备工作。

56. C 【解析】细节题。第一段第一句就指出并不是所有的旅游都能获得快乐。所以应选C。

57. C 【解析】细节题。文章讲旺季旅游很贵，而且很拥挤。所以应选 C。

58. A 【解析】细节题。根据“Any trip can be enjoyable if it is well planned.”可知事先计划好就会更愉快。所以应选A。

59. D 【解析】细节题。文章最后一段讲了需要准备的材料，可知申请护照时职业资格是不需要的。所以应选D。

60. C 【解析】推断题。文章讲了要出国就要准备护照，而这可能要花费一段时间，因此要提前准

56. C 【解析】细节题。第一段第一句就指出并不是所有的旅游都能获得快乐。所以应选C。

备。所以应选C。

61.【译文】它们是与教会挂钩的小机构，主要目的是培养学生的道德品行。【解析】这是一个含有定语从句的复合句，由 whose 引导。

62.【译文】从19世纪中叶到世纪末，有9,000多名美国青年因不满国内所受的教育而赴德深造。【解析】这是一个简单句，其中插入的过去分词短语在句子中作原因状语。

63.【译文】聘用教授的依据是他们在学科方面的知识，而不是正确的信仰和约束学生的强硬手段。

【解析】这是一个含有原因状语从句的复合句。

64.【译文】新的原则是大学既要传播知识也要创造知识。这就需要由学者型老师组成教工队伍。

【解析】这是一个含有表语从句的复合句，而且表语从句又由两个并列的分句组成。

65.【译文】哈佛大学的校长率先推出选课制度，这样学生们就能选择自己的专业。主修领域的概念也出现了。【解析】这是两个句子。第一个句子中含有一个由“介词+关系代词”引导的定语从句，由 by which 引导。

##### Section IV Writing

##### 解题步骤：

第一步：审题

1. 题型：提纲作文

2. 文体：议论文

第二步：框架分析

1. 一般文章格式

2. 点出每个要点

第三步：列出写作思路

1. 指出原因与所带来的问题

2. 给出解决办法

第四步：列出关键词和词组

become a source of headache; becoming more and more serious; the main reason for; the number of private cars; infrastructure construction can't keep up with its growth; leads to the heavy traffic congestion; influencing people's daily life and the city's economic development; in order to solve this problem; given top priority to the problem; given restrictions to the use of private cars, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

More than ever before, heavy traffic has become a source of headache for most people. Now it is becoming more and more serious. The main reason for this is that more and more people begin to use private cars. In recent years, the number of private cars is growing faster and faster. At the same time, the infrastructure construction can't keep up with its growth. Then it leads to the heavy traffic congestion.

It is influencing people's daily life and the city's economic development. For example, people have to start early in the morning to avoid being late for work. And in rush hours, they have to wait long time to go home. This both wastes time and exhausts people.

In order to solve this problem, the government has given top priority to the problem and a great number of solutions have been offered. Some people suggest that more streets and roads should be constructed. Some advise people to use buses instead of private cars. And in some cities the government has given restrictions to the use of private cars.

I believe our mutual effort will eventually solve this problem, and people will live more happily without this headache problem.

1. 题型：提纲作文

2. 文体：议论文

1. 一般文章格式

2. 点出每个要点

第三步：列出写作思路

2. 给出解决办法

第四步：列出关键词和词组

become a source of headache; becoming more and more serious; the main reason for; the number of private cars; infrastructure construction can't keep up with its growth; leads to the heavy traffic congestion; influencing people's daily life and the city's economic development; in order to solve this problem; given top priority to the problem; given restrictions to the use of private cars, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

More than ever before, heavy traffic has become a source of headache for most people. Now it is becoming more and more serious. The main reason for this is that more and more people begin to use private cars. In recent years, the number of private cars is growing faster and faster. At the same time, the infrastructure construction can't keep up with its growth. Then it leads to the heavy traffic congestion.

It is influencing people's daily life and the city's economic development. For example, people have to start early in the morning to avoid being late for work. And in rush hours, they have to wait long time to go home. This both wastes time and exhausts people.

In order to solve this problem, the government has given top priority to the problem and a great number of solutions have been offered. Some people suggest that more streets and roads should be constructed. Some advise people to use buses instead of private cars. And in some cities the government has given restrictions to the use of private cars.

I believe our mutual effort will eventually solve this problem, and people will live more happily without this headache problem.

# 2007年9月全国英语等级考试真题

#### Section I Listening Comprehension

1. accounts 【解析】细节题。根据“Accountants spend an average of five hours and fifteen minutes of their leisure time reading every week.”可知正确答案。

2. biographies 【解析】细节题。根据“Politicians who read for just under five hours a week favor biographies and history books.”可知正确答案。

3. forty-six 【解析】细节题。根据 “Taxi drivers manage an average of four hours and forty-six minutes a week…” 可知正确答案。

4. lawyers 【解析】细节题。根据“Lawyers read the highest percentage of crime fiction in their weekly average of four hours and thirty three minutes.”可知正确答案。

5. contemporary 【解析】细节题。根据“Teachers' strong preference is for contemporary fiction over the classics ..."可知正确答案。

6. understanding 【解析】细节题。根据“... most importantly, he should be understanding.”可知正确答案。

7. evenings and weekends 【解析】细节题。根据 “What work hours do you keep?” “Evenings and weekends.” 可知正确答案。

8. training opportunities 【解析】细节题。根据“There is a clear path overseas, because there are training opportunities unlike in Hong Kong.”可知正确答案。

9. beaten so much 【解析】细节题。根据“Dealing with dogs that have been beaten so much that they lose all their trust in people.”可知正确答案。

10. 10,000 HK dollars 【解析】细节题。根据“But a paid job starts at about seven thousand HK dollars without a degree and about ten thousand HK dollars with a degree.”可知正确答案。

11. C 【解析】细节题。根据“John Stewart Mill, the nineteenth century British philosopher”可知本题答案为 C。

12. A 【解析】细节题。对话 “They are usually first born children of middle-class families”, 可知本题答案为 A。其他选项与事实正好相反。

13. B 【解析】推断题。根据全文内容，可推断出正确答案为 B。

14. A 【解析】细节题。文中说他遇到 William 之后才意外地开始了歌唱生涯，故正确答案为 A。

15. B 【解析】细节题。根据“But when you get to forty, it’s just extraordinary. Apparently, the whole world opens up.”可知正确答案为 B。

16. B 【解析】细节题。根据“I’d love to learn how to play the violin...”可知正确答案为B。

17. D 【解析】细节题。根据“The prevailing attitude about premarital contracting is that it spoils the whole concept of marriage, love, and trust.”可知正确答案为D。

18. A 【解析】细节题。根据“But in fact, premarital contracting is a way that we can learn more about each other…”可知本题答案为A。

19. A 【解析】细节题。根据“Each year, there is one divorce for every two marriages.”可知本题答案为 A。

20. D 【解析】推断题。根据“Premarital contracting is a communication process...”可推断出最重要的是沟通，故本题选D。

### 2007年9月全国英语等级考试真题

#### Section I Listening Comprehension

##### Part A

1. accounts 【解析】细节题。根据“Accountants spend an average of five hours and fifteen minutes of their leisure time reading every week.”可知正确答案。

2. biographies 【解析】细节题。根据“Politicians who read for just under five hours a week favor biographies and history books.”可知正确答案。

3. forty-six 【解析】细节题。根据 “Taxi drivers manage an average of four hours and forty-six minutes a week…” 可知正确答案。

4. lawyers 【解析】细节题。根据“Lawyers read the highest percentage of crime fiction in their weekly average of four hours and thirty three minutes.”可知正确答案。

5. contemporary 【解析】细节题。根据“Teachers’ strong preference is for contemporary fiction over the classics …”可知正确答案。

##### Part B

6. understanding 【解析】细节题。根据“... most importantly, he should be understanding.”可知正确答案。

7. evenings and weekends 【解析】细节题。根据 “What work hours do you keep?” “Evenings and weekends.” 可知正确答案。

8. training opportunities 【解析】细节题。根据“There is a clear path overseas, because there are training opportunities unlike in Hong Kong.”可知正确答案。

9. beaten so much 【解析】细节题。根据“Dealing with dogs that have been beaten so much that they lose all their trust in people.”可知正确答案。

10. 10,000 HK dollars 【解析】细节题。根据“But a paid job starts at about seven thousand HK dollars without a degree and about ten thousand HK dollars with a degree.”可知正确答案。

##### Part C

11. C 【解析】细节题。根据“John Stewart Mill, the nineteenth century British philosopher”可知本题答案为 C。

12. A 【解析】细节题。对话 “They are usually first born children of middle-class families”, 可知本题答案为 A。其他选项与事实正好相反。

13. B 【解析】推断题。根据全文内容，可推断出正确答案为 B。

14. A 【解析】细节题。文中说他遇到 William 之后才意外地开始了歌唱生涯，故正确答案为 A。

15. B 【解析】细节题。根据“But when you get to forty, it’s just extraordinary. Apparently, the whole world opens up.”可知正确答案为 B。

16. B 【解析】细节题。根据“I’d love to learn how to play the violin...”可知正确答案为B。

17. D 【解析】细节题。根据“The prevailing attitude about premarital contracting is that it spoils the whole concept of marriage, love, and trust.”可知正确答案为D。

18. A 【解析】细节题。根据“But in fact, premarital contracting is a way that we can learn more about each other…”可知本题答案为A。

19. A 【解析】细节题。根据“Each year, there is one divorce for every two marriages.”可知本题答案为 A。

20. D 【解析】推断题。根据“Premarital contracting is a communication process…”可推断出最重要的是沟通，故本题选D。

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

Recently, a survey of the nation's reading habits was conducted in Britain. The survey asked members of different professions how much time they devoted to reading for pleasure every week. The researchers also asked what types of books they enjoyed. Accountants spend an average of five hours and fifteen minutes of their leisure time reading every week. They read all types of fiction, mainly in bed or while commuting to work. Secretaries spend an average of almost five hours reading every week with Jane Austin on top of their list. Politicians who read for just under five hours a week favor biographies and history books. Most of their reading is done in bed. Taxi drivers manage an average of four hours and forty-six minutes a week much of it while waiting in their cabs. They read more self-help books than other workers. Lawyers read the highest percentage of crime fiction in their weekly average of four hours and thirty three minutes. They have no interest in poetry, gardening, self-help books, or romance. Teachers read for an average of four hours and twenty seven minutes a week mainly in bed or on holiday. Teachers' strong preference is for contemporary fiction over the classics, but their favorite author is Jane Austin. A total of one thousand six hundred people were surveyed. The average time spent reading for pleasure was five hours and nine minutes a week.

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

M: What qualifications do you need?

W: I have a degree and worked for the SPCA for almost seven years. SPCA is a worldwide association that provides specialized care to pets. I graduated as a certified dog trainer and behavior council from the dog academy of San Francisco SPCA.

M: What sort of person does the job suit?

W: Of course, the person should not be afraid of dogs. But most importantly, he should be understanding.

M: What is the best way into the industry?

W: Try volunteering as a dog walker and a class assistant with dog academy certified trainers at Hong Kong SPCA. And you will learn a lot.

M: What work hours do you keep?

W: Evenings and weekends.

M: Is there a clear career path?

W: There is a clear path overseas, because there are training opportunities unlike in Hong Kong. It is a very specialized skill. Otherwise, if you like animals in general, there are many related careers like animal boarding, dog walking, pet-sitting, etc.

M: What's the best part of your job?

W: Being able to help people build relationships with their dogs, and training dogs using more gentle methods, preventing and resolving behavioral problems.

M: What's the worst?

W: Dealing with dogs that have been beaten so much that they lose all their trust in people.

M: Who is your role model?

W: I have two: renowned animal behaviorist, Doctor Ian Dunbar, and Jane Dennison, the director of the Academy at San Francisco SPCA.

M: Salary?

W: Mine is between twenty thousand and forty thousand HK dollars a month. But a paid job starts at about seven thousand HK dollars without a degree and about ten thousand HK dollars with a degree.

M: Last word.

W: Working with animals is an interesting and rewarding field to be in, but the pay may not be as good as one hopes.

##### 附：听力原文

听第1段材料，回答第1至5题。

Recently, a survey of the nation's reading habits was conducted in Britain. The survey asked members of different professions how much time they devoted to reading for pleasure every week. The researchers also asked what types of books they enjoyed. Accountants spend an average of five hours and fifteen minutes of their leisure time reading every week. They read all types of fiction, mainly in bed or while commuting to work. Secretaries spend an average of almost five hours reading every week with Jane Austin on top of their list. Politicians who read for just under five hours a week favor biographies and history books. Most of their reading is done in bed. Taxi drivers manage an average of four hours and forty-six minutes a week much of it while waiting in their cabs. They read more self-help books than other workers. Lawyers read the highest percentage of crime fiction in their weekly average of four hours and thirty three minutes. They have no interest in poetry, gardening, self-help books, or romance. Teachers read for an average of four hours and twenty seven minutes a week mainly in bed or on holiday. Teachers' strong preference is for contemporary fiction over the classics, but their favorite author is Jane Austin. A total of one thousand six hundred people were surveyed. The average time spent reading for pleasure was five hours and nine minutes a week.

听第2段材料，回答第6至10题。

M: What qualifications do you need?

W: I have a degree and worked for the SPCA for almost seven years. SPCA is a worldwide association that provides specialized care to pets. I graduated as a certified dog trainer and behavior council from the dog academy of San Francisco SPCA.

M: What sort of person does the job suit?

W: Of course, the person should not be afraid of dogs. But most importantly, he should be understanding.

M: What is the best way into the industry?

W: Try volunteering as a dog walker and a class assistant with dog academy certified trainers at Hong Kong SPCA. And you will learn a lot.

M: What work hours do you keep?

W: Evenings and weekends.

M: Is there a clear career path?

W: There is a clear path overseas, because there are training opportunities unlike in Hong Kong. It is a very specialized skill. Otherwise, if you like animals in general, there are many related careers like animal boarding, dog walking, pet-sitting, etc.

M: What's the best part of your job?

W: Being able to help people build relationships with their dogs, and training dogs using more gentle methods, preventing and resolving behavioral problems.

M: What's the worst?

W: Dealing with dogs that have been beaten so much that they lose all their trust in people.

M: Who is your role model?

W: I have two: renowned animal behaviorist, Doctor Ian Dunbar, and Jane Dennison, the director of the Academy at San Francisco SPCA.

M: Salary?

W: Mine is between twenty thousand and forty thousand HK dollars a month. But a paid job starts at about seven thousand HK dollars without a degree and about ten thousand HK dollars with a degree.

M: Last word.

W: Working with animals is an interesting and rewarding field to be in, but the pay may not be as good as one hopes.

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

For centuries, people have been amazed by children of unusual talent. Pianist and composer Felix Mendelssohn has composed a fair amount of music by the time he was eleven. His forth opera was produced in Berlin, when he was only eighteen. John Stewart Mill, the nineteenth century British philosopher, read Greek at three and had worked his way through elementary geometry and a large body of literature and history by the time he was twelve. Even though there has been a fascination with prodigies for centuries, there has been little serious study of them until recently. Some surprising common characteristics have been identified. The vast majority are boys. They are usually first born children of middle-class families. Often their parents are past the usual childbearing age. Many are born by operation rather than by natural childbirth. They often have parents who seem to be trying to realize their own ambitions through their amazing children. And prodigies usually have a strange sense of humor. Although a child may be born with outstanding genetic potential, this potential will not necessarily develop. "Just having the gene is not enough," says Harvard University psychologist Howard Gardener. "Something in the environment must nourish the potential." And although many prodigies enjoy the satisfaction of the extraordinary achievement, public praise, and material wealth, even the most successful sometimes question the value of their lives and the accomplishment. "I have a longing which grows stronger as I get older." confesses the famous American concert pianist Eugene Eastman, "to be ordinary."

听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

M: You experimented with a lot of musical styles. What's next?

W: It's hard to say where I'm going next, because my next record isn't finished.

M: You used to go to acting classes before you got into music. Did you ever consider becoming an actress?

W: That's what I wanted to do initially. I left school and joined a traveling theater company. We didn't have money for hotels. So we used to camp in parks. It was brilliant. Then I met William. He liked my voice and decided I should be a singer. It was queer because singing was something I never had in mind.

M: Is it true that the best time of a woman's life is in her thirties?

W: Well. Someone's been telling me that it really starts at forty, she is a wonderful woman. And she says the 30s are just as hard as the 20s, but in a different way. They are just confusing. But when you get to forty, it's just extraordinary. Apparently, the whole world opens up.

M: What would you like to achieve before you're... say..., sixty?

W: I'd love to learn how to play the violin but not before I'm sixty. I'd like to do it in the next year or so. One of the first instruments I learned was the drums. And I am quite good at that coordination in a strange way.

听第 5 段材料，回答第 17 至 20 题。

M: The prevailing attitude about premarital contracting is that it spoils the whole concept of marriage, love, and trust. How do you counter that?

W: First of all, most of us hear about premarital contracting when someone rich and famous gets divorced and about all the battles they are going through. I know when my husband and I decided that we wanted to have a premarital contract, my son said "Why you guys doing that? You don't trust each other?" So it's not surprising that people's initial reaction is a negative one. But in fact, premarital contracting is a way that we can learn more about each other, make each other feel more comfortable about issues that are of concerns, and certainly clarify money concerns. This way, we can talk about them, decide together how we want our marriage to work, and if necessary, and only if necessary, we put it in a premarital contract, a legal document.

M: Each year, there is one divorce for every two marriages. And a substantial portion of those who have divorced remarry. Is the notion of the premarital contract simply for those who are entering second marriages, or is it also something for the people getting married for the first time?

## 全国英语 等级考试 全真 模拟试题第4级

听第3段材料，回答第11至13题。

For centuries, people have been amazed by children of unusual talent. Pianist and composer Felix Mendelssohn has composed a fair amount of music by the time he was eleven. His forth opera was produced in Berlin, when he was only eighteen. John Stewart Mill, the nineteenth century British philosopher, read Greek at three and had worked his way through elementary geometry and a large body of literature and history by the time he was twelve. Even though there has been a fascination with prodigies for centuries, there has been little serious study of them until recently. Some surprising common characteristics have been identified. The vast majority are boys. They are usually first born children of middle-class families. Often their parents are past the usual childbearing age. Many are born by operation rather than by natural childbirth. They often have parents who seem to be trying to realize their own ambitions through their amazing children. And prodigies usually have a strange sense of humor. Although a child may be born with outstanding genetic potential, this potential will not necessarily develop. "Just having the gene is not enough," says Harvard University psychologist Howard Gardener. "Something in the environment must nourish the potential." And although many prodigies enjoy the satisfaction of the extraordinary achievement, public praise, and material wealth, even the most successful sometimes question the value of their lives and the accomplishment. "I have a longing which grows stronger as I get older." confesses the famous American concert pianist Eugene Eastman, "to be ordinary."

听第4段材料，回答第14至16题。

M: You experimented with a lot of musical styles. What's next?

W: It's hard to say where I'm going next, because my next record isn't finished.

M: You used to go to acting classes before you got into music. Did you ever consider becoming an actress?

W: That's what I wanted to do initially. I left school and joined a traveling theater company. We didn't have money for hotels. So we used to camp in parks. It was brilliant. Then I met William. He liked my voice and decided I should be a singer. It was queer because singing was something I never had in mind.

M: Is it true that the best time of a woman's life is in her thirties?

W: Well. Someone's been telling me that it really starts at forty, she is a wonderful woman. And she says the 30s are just as hard as the 20s, but in a different way. They are just confusing. But when you get to forty, it's just extraordinary. Apparently, the whole world opens up.

M: What would you like to achieve before you're... say..., sixty?

W: I'd love to learn how to play the violin but not before I'm sixty. I'd like to do it in the next year or so. One of the first instruments I learned was the drums. And I am quite good at that coordination in a strange way.

听第5段材料，回答第17至20题。

M: The prevailing attitude about premarital contracting is that it spoils the whole concept of marriage, love, and trust. How do you counter that?

W: First of all, most of us hear about premarital contracting when someone rich and famous gets divorced and about all the battles they are going through. I know when my husband and I decided that we wanted to have a premarital contract, my son said "Why you guys doing that? You don't trust each other?" So it's not surprising that people's initial reaction is a negative one. But in fact, premarital contracting is a way that we can learn more about each other, make each other feel more comfortable about issues that are of concerns, and certainly clarify money concerns. This way, we can talk about them, decide together how we want our marriage to work, and if necessary, and only if necessary, we put it in a premarital contract, a legal document.

M: Each year, there is one divorce for every two marriages. And a substantial portion of those who have divorced remarry. Is the notion of the premarital contract simply for those who are entering second marriages, or is it also something for the people getting married for the first time?

W: Premarital contracting is a communication process. I think all of us, whatever age we are, whatever financial status we have, we have things to talk about. And if we avoid doing that and wait until there are problems, it's almost too late. I think that no matter what age, whether we've been married once or not, whether we have stepchildren, whether we have assets, it's important to communicate and consider a legal document.

##### Section II Use of English

[参考译文] 信不信由你，航空公司确实正在努力做得更好。由于议会已经厌烦了噩梦般的飞行故事，所以去年，航空公司迫于议会的压力答应改善客服质量。

尽管航空公司花了大量的钱来改善服务，投入了大量资金购买新的设备，像移动验票站和便携式电话银行。有了这些设施，游客就可以在航班延误或取消时重新预定航班。事实上，乘飞机旅行一直让人很烦恼，1999年顾客向交通运输部提出的投诉比1998年增加了一倍。

今年，大自然似乎更愿意让人们乘公共汽车。最近，反常的坏天气——以长期的雷雨天气为特色，使机场瘫痪了，并导致了大范围的航班被延误和取消。去年夏天相似的问题出现之后，联邦航空部答应会对负责因天气引起的延误做出反应的航空公司做深入的调查。例如，联邦航空部和航空公司代表现在聚在 Herndon 的一个叫 Va.的地方，为的是要找出最好的方法分配可用的领空。但是联邦航空部也承认新的倡议有负众望，许多乘客抱怨航班延误好像是必然的。一部分问题是过度拥挤的飞机。基于雄厚的经济基础，人们期待美国航空公司今年能比去年的运输量提高5%，即运送655,000,000名乘客。平均76%的飞机是满员，这也是前所未有的。这对于美国交通运输局来说是个好消息，因为每架飞机载的乘客越多，他们获得的利润也就越大；但对于乘客来说却是个坏消息，因为拥挤的环境让人感到烦恼。

21. A 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。under pressure 为固定短语，表示“在压力下，被迫”。故选 A。

22. B 【解析】本题考查短语辨析。hold back，阻止；be fed up with，厌烦；take in by，受骗；fill in with 用…填满。由上下文可知，国会应该是厌倦了噩梦般的飞行故事，因此B项最符合题意。

23. B 【解析】本题考查形容词辨析。inferior，低等的；desperate，绝望的；fatal，致命的；worse，更差的。由下文"The fact is that air travel has... been such an annoyance, and..."可以看出，乘客的投诉越来越多，证明乘飞机旅行变得越来越糟，所以这里应该选B。

24. C 【解析】本题考查连词。as if，好像；so that，为的是…；even though，即使；now that，既然。由后文提到的航空公司所做的努力和变得更糟的结果来看，前后分句是转折关系，故选C。

25. D 【解析】本题考查动词辨析。upgrade，提高；purchase，购买；install，安装；invest，投资。invest in 为固定搭配，意为“买进”，故选 D。

26. D 【解析】本题考查动词辨析。reclaim，取回、拿回、回收；reserve，储备，保存；recall，回忆；rebook，重新预定。能接 a flight 作宾语的只有 rebook，故选 D。

27. D 【解析】本题考查上下文的理解。从本题的后半句可以看出，乘飞机旅行一直让人很烦恼，故选D。

28. A 【解析】本题考查连词。本句的意思是“从1998年以后，顾客对交通运输局的不满在1999年翻了一番”，因此since（从…以后）最合适。故选A。

29. C 【解析】本题考查上下文的理解。从后面反常的坏天气使机场瘫痪，延误了很多航班来看，大自然似乎更愿意人们乘公共汽车，故选 C。

30. A 【解析】本题考查动词的用法。feature 作为及物动词表示“以…为特色”；capture，捕获，获得；distinguish，区别，使显出特色，一般与 from 连用，即 distinguish A from B；characterize，以…为特征，一般用于被动语态。这里所说的坏天气主要是雷雨，故选 A。

31. B 【解析】本题考查短语。be charged with, 承担责任；respond to, 回答, 响应, 有反应；reply to, 回答, 答复；abide by, 遵守。本句中，airlines 和 weather slowdowns 之间是“航空公司负责天气引起延误”或“航空公司对天气引起的延误做出反应”，如果选 A，不应该是主动语态，故选 B。

### 参考答案与解析

## 143

W: Premarital contracting is a communication process. I think all of us, whatever age we are, whatever financial status we have, we have things to talk about. And if we avoid doing that and wait until there are problems, it's almost too late. I think that no matter what age, whether we've been married once or not, whether we have stepchildren, whether we have assets, it's important to communicate and consider a legal document.

##### Section II Use of English

[参考译文] 信不信由你，航空公司确实正在努力做得更好。由于议会已经厌烦了噩梦般的飞行故事，所以去年，航空公司迫于议会的压力答应改善客服质量。

尽管航空公司花了大量的钱来改善服务，投入了大量资金购买新的设备，像移动验票站和便携式电话银行。有了这些设施，游客就可以在航班延误或取消时重新预定航班。事实上，乘飞机旅行一直让人很烦恼，1999年顾客向交通运输部提出的投诉比1998年增加了一倍。

今年，大自然似乎更愿意让人们乘公共汽车。最近，反常的坏天气——以长期的雷雨天气为特色，使机场瘫痪了，并导致了大范围的航班被延误和取消。去年夏天相似的问题出现之后，联邦航空部答应会对负责因天气引起的延误做出反应的航空公司做深入的调查。例如，联邦航空部和航空公司代表现在聚在 Herndon 的一个叫 Va.的地方，为的是要找出最好的方法分配可用的领空。但是联邦航空部也承认新的倡议有负众望，许多乘客抱怨航班延误好像是必然的。一部分问题是过度拥挤的飞机。基于雄厚的经济基础，人们期待美国航空公司今年能比去年的运输量提高5%，即运送655,000,000名乘客。平均76%的飞机是满员，这也是前所未有的。这对于美国交通运输局来说是个好消息，因为每架飞机载的乘客越多，他们获得的利润也就越大；但对于乘客来说却是个坏消息，因为拥挤的环境让人感到烦恼。

21. A 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。under pressure 为固定短语，表示“在压力下，被迫”。故选 A。

22. B 【解析】本题考查短语辨析。hold back，阻止；be fed up with，厌烦；take in by，受骗；fill in with 用…填满。由上下文可知，国会应该是厌倦了噩梦般的飞行故事，因此B项最符合题意。

23. B 【解析】本题考查形容词辨析。inferior，低等的；desperate，绝望的；fatal，致命的；worse，更差的。由下文"The fact is that air travel has... been such an annoyance, and..."可以看出，乘客的投诉越来越多，证明乘飞机旅行变得越来越糟，所以这里应该选B。

24. C 【解析】本题考查连词。as if，好像；so that，为的是…；even though，即使；now that，既然。由后文提到的航空公司所做的努力和变得更糟的结果来看，前后分句是转折关系，故选C。

25. D 【解析】本题考查动词辨析。upgrade，提高；purchase，购买；install，安装；invest，投资。invest in 为固定搭配，意为“买进”，故选 D。

26. D 【解析】本题考查动词辨析。reclaim，取回、拿回、回收；reserve，储备，保存；recall，回忆；rebook，重新预定。能接 a flight 作宾语的只有 rebook，故选 D。

27. D 【解析】本题考查上下文的理解。从本题的后半句可以看出，乘飞机旅行一直让人很烦恼，故选D。

28. A 【解析】本题考查连词。本句的意思是“从1998年以后，顾客对交通运输局的不满在1999年翻了一番”，因此since（从…以后）最合适。故选A。

29. C 【解析】本题考查上下文的理解。从后面反常的坏天气使机场瘫痪，延误了很多航班来看，大自然似乎更愿意人们乘公共汽车，故选 C。

30. A 【解析】本题考查动词的用法。feature 作为及物动词表示“以…为特色”；capture，捕获，获得；distinguish，区别，使显出特色，一般与 from 连用，即 distinguish A from B；characterize，以…为特征，一般用于被动语态。这里所说的坏天气主要是雷雨，故选 A。

31. B 【解析】本题考查短语。be charged with, 承担责任；respond to, 回答, 响应, 有反应；reply to, 回答, 答复；abide by, 遵守。本句中，airlines 和 weather slowdowns 之间是“航空公司负责天气引起延误”或“航空公司对天气引起的延误做出反应”，如果选 A，不应该是主动语态，故选 B。

32. C 【解析】本题考查短语动词。draw in, 拉进, 拉人, 诱使某人参与; follow up, 追究, 追查; figure out, 想出; set aside, 留出。这里是说航空公司的代表要想出最好的方法分配可用的领空, 故选 C。

33. A 【解析】本题考查动词词义辨析。admit，承认；allow，允许；reveal，揭示；claim，宣布。前句话说联邦航空部做了努力，本句和前句是转折关系，意思是即便如此，就连联邦航空部也承认新的倡议有负众望，故选 A。

34. D 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。lag，滞后，延迟；lack，欠缺，不足，作为动词通常以 lack in...的形式出现，作为名词通常以 lack of...的形式出现；scarce，缺乏的，不足的，一般作表语，如 be scarce；fall short of 为固定搭配，意为“未达到目的”。故选 D 项。

35. A 【解析】本题考查形容词辨析。absolute，绝对的，必然的；arbitrary，任意的；plentiful，充足的；superfluous 过剩的，过多的。that 后的从句作 complain 的宾语，由前一分句可知，许多乘客抱怨航班延误好像是“必然的”，故选 A。

36. D 【解析】本题考查短语。according to, 根据；except for, 除了…；thanks to, 幸亏；based on, 基于…。本句意为“基于雄厚的经济基础，人们期望美国航空公司今年能达到运输乘客达655,000,000名的记录。”故选D项。

37. B 【解析】本题考查固定搭配。本题是对 on average 这个固定短语的考查。故选 B。

38. D 【解析】本题考查名词辨析。fantasy, 想象, 幻想；monument, 纪念品；legend, 传说；record, 记录, 最高成绩。根据题意，现在飞机平均达到了76%的载客率，这也是一个记录，故选D。

39. D 【解析】本题考查介词的用法。本题考查与 flight 搭配的介词，表示“在每一航班上”，只能用 on。故选 D。

40. A 【解析】本题考查连词。since，因为…；although，虽然，即使；unless，除非；if，如果。这句话是对前半句的解释说明，两句话是因果关系，所以选 A。

##### Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Text 1

[文章概述] 本文先讲到太空探索的未来取决于很多因素，然后做了详细说明。

41. A 【解析】细节题。从第一段最后一句话 “Any flight to other solar systems is even more distant, but a huge advance in space technology could drive space exploration into realms currently explored only by science fiction.” 可看出，技术上的突破才能使进入太阳系成为可能，B、C、D 三项只是影响太空探索的未来的因素。故选 A。

42. D 【解析】推断题。从第二段最后一句话“... but the 21st century will see a continuation of efforts to transform humanity into a space-faring species.”可知，人类一直进行着太空探索。这与D项所表达的意思一致。所以应选D。

43. C 【解析】判断题。第三段说探索太空的专家们正面临着如何降低太空飞行的成本问题，因此载人太空任务只能在成本大大降低时才能成为可能。只有 C 项最为贴切。所以应选 C。

44. B 【解析】细节题。文章第四段第二句话指出人类要想在其他星球上生活就必须在艰苦的环境中获得生活必需品。只有 B 项表达了这个意思。所以应选 B。

45. B 【解析】细节题。由文章第三段“Perhaps the most difficult problem space planners face is how to finance a vigorous program of piloted space exploration...”可知，人类抵达其他星球的最大问题是资金问题，故选 B。

##### Text 2

【文章概述】本文主要讲述了不同人种、不同性别的人之间存在的工资差异。

##### Section III Reading Comprehension

##### Text 1

[文章概述] 本文先讲到太空探索的未来取决于很多因素，然后做了详细说明。

41. A 【解析】细节题。从第一段最后一句话 “Any flight to other solar systems is even more distant, but a huge advance in space technology could drive space exploration into realms currently explored only by science fiction.” 可看出，技术上的突破才能使进入太阳系成为可能，B、C、D 三项只是影响太空探索的未来的因素。故选 A。

42. D 【解析】推断题。从第二段最后一句话“… but the 21st century will see a continuation of efforts to transform humanity into a space-faring species.”可知，人类一直进行着太空探索。这与D项所表达的意思一致。所以应选D。

43. C 【解析】判断题。第三段说探索太空的专家们正面临着如何降低太空飞行的成本问题，因此载人太空任务只能在成本大大降低时才能成为可能。只有 C 项最为贴切。所以应选 C。

44. B 【解析】细节题。文章第四段第二句话指出人类要想在其他星球上生活就必须在艰苦的环境中获得生活必需品。只有 B 项表达了这个意思。所以应选 B。

45. B 【解析】细节题。由文章第三段“Perhaps the most difficult problem space planners face is how to finance a vigorous program of piloted space exploration...”可知，人类抵达其他星球的最大问题是资金问题，故选 B。

##### Text 2

【文章概述】本文主要讲述了不同人种、不同性别的人之间存在的工资差异。

46. C 【解析】细节题。从文章第一段第三句话 “... women, traditionally secondary earners, assumed greater responsibility for their own and their children's well-being.” 中可以看出，20世纪80年代女性开始在养家方面起到了重要的作用。只有C项符合题意。所以应选C。

47. A 【解析】细节题。文章第二段第二句话对白人女性、白人男性、有色人种的女性、有色人种的男性的工资做了比较：“White women are more than three times as likely as white men to be low wage workers. And black or Hispanic men more than one and a half times as likely.” 比较之下只有有色人种的女性可能是最贫穷的。答案 A 符合题意。所以应选 A。

48. C 【解析】细节题。从第三段可以看出，有工作不再意味着就可以摆脱贫穷，孩子们不可能因为低收入的父母而变得富有，可见 C 项符合题意。所以应选 C。

49. B 【解析】语义题。第五段第一句话中的 unionization 本身表示的是“联合”，只有 B 项的意思最贴切。故选 B。

50. B 【解析】主旨题。这篇文章第一段第二句话就指出“It is a vital step toward overcoming inequality in American society.”而且通篇都在讲不同人种之间工资的差异，所以本文的主题应选B项，即工人工资之间的不平等性。故应选B。

##### Text 3

【文章概述】本文对社会学和教育学先驱杜·波依斯进行了介绍。

51. B 【解析】理解题。由文章第四段第二句话“The major difference between the two men was that Washington sought change…”可知，杜·波依斯的政治立场比华盛顿的更彻底、更激进，故选B。

52. D 【解析】细节题。文中有提到杜·波依斯编过杂志，是教育学先驱，有硕士学位，但文中没有提到杜·波依斯是政府官员，因而选D。

53. C 【解析】观点态度题。文章第三段第四句话“In terms of its educational policy, the NAACP position was that all American children and youth should have genuine equality of educational opportunity.”可以看出他认为每个人都有受教育的权利。故选C。

54. A 【解析】判断题。由文章最后一段：“... Washington sought change that was evolutionary in nature and did not upset the social order...” 可知，华盛顿不赞同推翻社会秩序，overthrow 的意思同 upset，意为“推翻”，故应选 A。

55. B 【解析】推断题。文章最后一段说根据杜·波依斯的“有才华的10%”的概念，10%的黑人会接受传统的大学教育，从而为成为未来的领导人员做准备，并没有表明这些人已经准备好，故A不正确；C项“华盛顿和杜·波依斯从来都不是朋友”，文中没有提到，故也不正确；D项“仅仅只有10%的人值得受教育”与杜·波依斯提出的人人享有平等的教育机会的观点相悖。故应选B。

##### Text 4

【文章概述】本文主要讲述了美国政府在吸引外国人才和发放“绿卡”方面应采用的一些方法和原则。

56. C 【解析】推断题。从文章第一段第一句话 “Our analysis therefore suggests that the real problem facing the black community lies in educational obstacles prior to the Ph.D. programs rather than in the pour—in of foreign students.” 可以看出，作者在写这篇文章之前做了一项关于黑人教育问题的分析，所以选 C。

57. C 【解析】推断题。从文章第二段最后一句话“We suggest extending the idea from financial to human capital.”可以看出，作者认为外国博士生也是一种资本。故选 C。

58. A 【解析】细节题。文章第三段提到在美国毕业的外国博士生必须得到雇主的保证才能继续留在美国，只有A项符合这个意思。故选A。

59. A 【解析】细节题。从文章第四段最后一句话 “it unfairly penalizes smaller firms that cannotafford this expensive process...” 可以看出小公司不能承担引进人才的昂贵费用，所以选 A。

52. D 【解析】细节题。文中有提到杜·波依斯编过杂志，是教育学先驱，有硕士学位，但文中没有提到杜·波依斯是政府官员，因而选D。

53. C 【解析】观点态度题。文章第三段第四句话“In terms of its educational policy, the NAACP position was that all American children and youth should have genuine equality of educational opportunity.”可以看出他认为每个人都有受教育的权利。故选C。

54. A 【解析】判断题。由文章最后一段：“... Washington sought change that was evolutionary in nature and did not upset the social order...” 可知，华盛顿不赞同推翻社会秩序，overthrow 的意思同 upset，意为“推翻”，故应选 A。

55. B 【解析】推断题。文章最后一段说根据杜·波依斯的“有才华的10%”的概念，10%的黑人会接受传统的大学教育，从而为成为未来的领导人员做准备，并没有表明这些人已经准备好，故A不正确；C项“华盛顿和杜·波依斯从来都不是朋友”，文中没有提到，故也不正确；D项“仅仅只有10%的人值得受教育”与杜·波依斯提出的人人享有平等的教育机会的观点相悖。故应选B。

##### Text 4

【文章概述】本文主要讲述了美国政府在吸引外国人才和发放“绿卡”方面应采用的一些方法和原则。

56. C 【解析】推断题。从文章第一段第一句话 “Our analysis therefore suggests that the real problem facing the black community lies in educational obstacles prior to the Ph.D. programs rather than in the pour—in of foreign students.” 可以看出，作者在写这篇文章之前做了一项关于黑人教育问题的分析，所以选 C。

57. C 【解析】推断题。从文章第二段最后一句话“We suggest extending the idea from financial to human capital.”可以看出，作者认为外国博士生也是一种资本。故选 C。

58. A 【解析】细节题。文章第三段提到在美国毕业的外国博士生必须得到雇主的保证才能继续留在美国，只有A项符合这个意思。故选A。

59. A 【解析】细节题。从文章第四段最后一句话 “it unfairly penalizes smaller firms that cannotafford this expensive process…” 可以看出小公司不能承担引进人才的昂贵费用，所以选 A。

60. D 【解析】细节题。由文章第五段和第六段可知，1990年的《美国移民归化法案》只是缩短了申请的时间，而作者却提议主动授予那些在美国大学里获得科学和工程学博士学位的人“绿卡”，故D项最符合题意。故选D。

61.【译文】悉尼正在准备通过举办这样的国际盛会而收获同洛杉矶一样的经济利益，1984年的洛杉矶奥运会使洛杉矶成为了举办奥运会以来获利最多的一个城市。【解析】这是一个含有定语从句的复合句。最后的名词短语是作前面一词的同位语。

62.【译文】但是，如果悉尼失去了主办机会，新南威尔士州和澳大利亚的纳税人会赞同政府花上百万美元在一次失败而昂贵的练习上吗？【解析】这是一个含有条件状语从句的复合句。

63.【译文】联邦政府批准的500万美元，实际上意味着悉尼申奥得到了每位澳大利亚纳税人的经济支持。【解析】这是一个含有宾语从句的复合句。

64.【译文】其他人认为70%的设备已经到位了，而且他们都处于政府所有的土地上，这就消除了困扰以前申奥的潜在地区冲突。【解析】这是一个含有两个并列宾语从句的复合句，而且第二个宾语从句中含有一个由关系词 which 引导的定语从句修饰前面的先行词。

65.【译文】除了其他更明显的原因，像修建体育场、旅游业等，我认为真正的意义在于心理的变化——获得信心。【解析】这是一个含有宾语从句的复合句，但省略了连词 that。

#### Section IV Writing

##### 解题步骤：

第一步：审题

1. 题型：主题句作文

2. 文体：议论文

第二步：框架分析

1. 一般文章格式

2. 点出每个要点

第三步：列出写作思路

1. 读书与思想之间的关系

2. 举例说明

3. 总结结论

第四步：列出关键词和词组

think independently; creative talents; the key factor for development; the source of thinking; provides us a basis for thinking and a stand for judgment; the more a man reads, the better a man thinks; set a good example for us; can succeed without quick mind, etc.

##### 第五步：落笔成文

“The man who reads well is the man who thinks well, who has a background for opinion and a stand for judgment.” From this statement we can see that reading is essential for thinking.

We are encouraged to think independently from early ages. Nowadays the creative talents are needed badly. Creativity is advocated as the key factor for development. It seems that thinking is more important. But what is the source of thinking? Only by knowing more can we think more deeply? Reading provides us a basis for thinking and a stand for judgment. The more a man reads, the better a man thinks.

Confucius sets a good example for us. By reading extensively, he set up the Confucian school. Confucianism has been the most influencing ideology in China. All the thoughts and opinions of Confucius must have sources—reading.

To sum up, both thinking and reading are important, but reading is more important for it is the basis of thinking. No one can succeed without quick mind. But no one can have a quick mind without reading more.

I have already got a job.

My major is law.

I enjoy analyzing the cases most.

I like Civil Law best.

- What job do you do?

I am a teacher.

- Do you like it? And why?

Yes, I like it very much. Because I think I can realize my self-value and I like the students very much.

#### 口试真题与提示（一）

#### Part A (2 minutes)

##### Interlocutor:

- Good morning/afternoon. Could I have your mark sheets, please? Thank you.

(Hand over the mark sheets to the A

- My name is... and this is my colleague... He/she will just listen to us. So, you are... (name) and... (name) ? Thank you.

- First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask you some questions. (Select two or more questions from each of the following categories for Candidate A.)

##### Hometown

– What can you tell me about your hometown?

My hometown is a beautiful small village in Hebei Province.

##### Work/Study

- Are you still a student or have you already got a job?

I have already got a job.

(To a student)

– What do you specialize in?

My major is law.

– What do you enjoy most about your studies?

I enjoy analyzing the cases most.

– What subject(s) do you like best?

I like Civil Law best.

- Have you ever worked during the vacation?

Yes, I have.

– What kind of job did you do?

I worked as a part-time teacher.

- How did you like it?

I found it very interesting.

(To an adult who has already got a job)

- What job do you do?

I am a teacher.

- Do you like it? And why?

Yes, I like it very much. Because I think I can realize my self-value and I like the students very much.

– What qualifications did you need in order to get your “job”?

First I must have a good knowledge of the subject and also other knowledge, then I also need communication and speech skills, and most importantly I must love my students.

(Select two or more questions from each of the following categories for Candidate B.)

##### Family

- What can you tell me about your family?

I have a big family. There are seven people in my family.

##### Leisure

- Do you have any hobbies?

Yes, I like collecting stamps.

- How did you become interested in... (whatever hobby the candidate enjoys)?

I found it very interesting and could learn much knowledge through this, and then I develop this hobby.

– Which do you prefer, watching TV or going to the cinema? What sort of program/film do you like to watch?

I prefer watching TV. I like to watch programs on nature and science.

- What kinds of sports are you interested in? Why?

I'm interested in jogging, because it is very easy and cheap, and it can keep me healthy.

- What kinds of music do you enjoy most? Why?

I like light music, because it can make me fully relax after a day's work.

- How do you usually spend your holidays?

I usually spend my holidays at home, accompanying my families.

- Is there anyplace you would particularly like to visit? Why?

Yes, I particularly like to visit Tibet, because I think it is very mysterious.

#### Part B (3 minutes)

##### Interlocutor:

- Now I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves. Please speak loudly so that we can hear you. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

(Put the Picture for Candidates in front of both candidates and give instructions with reference to the picture.)

- If you need a place to live in, you can either rent an apartment or buy one on bank loan. Make your choice and give your reasons.

- This picture is for your reference. You have three minutes for this. Would you like to begin now, please?

##### Candidates:

A: In the picture we can see that we have to borrow a large sum of money from the bank in order to buy a house.

B: Yes, and people need to pay a certain amount of money each month. I think it is really a burden for a family.

A: Right. But we can also see with a comparatively small sum of money people can rent an apartment.

So I prefer renting an apartment to buying a house. It's cheap and most people can afford it.

B: It's true. But the apartment isn't your property, so you have to move often. Then I would like to buy a house, because once you buy it, it belongs to you.

# Part C (7 minutes)

##### Interlocutor:

- I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first describe it briefly and then give your comment on what you see in the picture.

(Put picture 1 for Candidates in front of both candidates.)

– … (name of Candidate A), this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

- ... (name of Candidate B), listen carefully while... (Name of Candidate A) is speaking. When he/she has finished, I'd like you to ask him/her a question about what he/she has said.

– … (name of Candidate A), would you like to begin now, please?

Candidate A: (3 minutes)

As can be seen in the picture, cheating in exams is a common phenomenon in school. In order to eliminate this phenomenon, the school has adopted very strict measures. The notice is clear evidence. The notice on the wall tells us that students who cheat in exams shall be expelled from school. The notice is hung on the outside of the examination room to warn the students of the serious results.

This fully reveals the seriousness of cheating in exams in school. Though the school will give severe punishment to students who cheat in exams, this phenomenon seems inevitable in school. If we don't eliminate this phenomenon, exams will certainly become unfair for non-cheating students. And soon the majority of the students will learn and choose to cheat in exams so as to "follow the style". And it also harms the cheated students, because if they get what they want through cheating in exams, they will not study hard in the future, and then they will learn nothing and become dishonest. This is bad for their learning as well as their growth. So I think it is right for school to adopt these strict measures.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, (name of Candidate B), could you please ask your partner a question?

(Half a minute for asking and answering the question.)

Candidate B: Don't you think the measure is too severe for students?

Candidate A: Maybe you can say it is very severe. But even if we adopt this severe measure, cheating in exams still exits in schools. And I think they bear the punishment resulting from their own mistakes.

(Take back Picture 1 and put Picture 2 for Candidates in front of both candidates.)

This fully reveals the seriousness of cheating in exams in school. Though the school will give severe punishment to students who cheat in exams, this phenomenon seems inevitable in school. If we don't eliminate this phenomenon, exams will certainly become unfair for non-cheating students. And soon the majority of the students will learn and choose to cheat in exams so as to "follow the style". And it also harms the cheated students, because if they get what they want through cheating in exams, they will not study hard in the future, and then they will learn nothing and become dishonest. - Thank you. Now, (name of Candidate B), could you please ask your partner a question?

Candidate B: Don't you think the measure is too severe for students?

Candidate A: Maybe you can say it is very severe. But even if we adopt this severe measure, cheating in exams still exits in schools. And I think they bear the punishment resulting from their own mistakes.

- OK. (name of Candidate B), here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

- ... (name of Candidate A), listen carefully while... (name of Candidate B) is speaking. When he/she has finished, I'd like you to ask him/her a question about what he/she has said.

– … (name of Candidate B), would you like to begin now, please?

##### Candidate B: (about 3 minutes)

The picture shows us a serious phenomenon at present. The officer in charge of recruiting new soldiers has waited for three days there, but no one signs up. The slogan "Joining the army a glory, recruiting new soldiers" is hung behind the three young people, but they don't pay attention to it at all. And they wear an expression of scorn on their face.

As can be seen in the picture, the awareness of joining the army is becoming dimmer and dimmer among today's young people. They not only don't want to join the army, but also scorn and laugh at it. This is a very serious problem that needs attention. If we don't cultivate the idea of joining the army is a glory, the young will be reluctant to serve in the army more and more. This is not only bad for the young, but also affects our country. The young will lose the chance to exercise in the army and our army will be short of new blood. So we should do something to educate the young, making them know the importance and benefit of serving in the army.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, (name of Candidate A), could you please ask your partner a question?

(Half a minute for asking and answering the question.)

Candidate A: What do you think has caused this problem?

Candidate B: I think it is because the young have too comfortable life nowadays. They are brought up as the “little emperor” in their family. So they don’t want to be trained and exercised in the army. It’s also because they don’t have a correct idea about the army and joining the army.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. That is the end of the test.

## 全国英语 等级考试 全真 模拟试题第4级

## 150

- OK. (name of Candidate B), here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

- ... (name of Candidate A), listen carefully while... (name of Candidate B) is speaking. When he/she has finished, I'd like you to ask him/her a question about what he/she has said.

– … (name of Candidate B), would you like to begin now, please?

##### Candidate B: (about 3 minutes)

The picture shows us a serious phenomenon at present. The officer in charge of recruiting new soldiers has waited for three days there, but no one signs up. The slogan "Joining the army a glory, recruiting new soldiers" is hung behind the three young people, but they don't pay attention to it at all. And they wear an expression of scorn on their face.

As can be seen in the picture, the awareness of joining the army is becoming dimmer and dimmer among today's young people. They not only don't want to join the army, but also scorn and laugh at it. This is a very serious problem that needs attention. If we don't cultivate the idea of joining the army is a glory, the young will be reluctant to serve in the army more and more. This is not only bad for the young, but also affects our country. The young will lose the chance to exercise in the army and our army will be short of new blood. So we should do something to educate the young, making them know the importance and benefit of serving in the army.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, (name of Candidate A), could you please ask your partner a question?

(Half a minute for asking and answering the question.)

Candidate A: What do you think has caused this problem?

Candidate B: I think it is because the young have too comfortable life nowadays. They are brought up as the “little emperor” in their family. So they don’t want to be trained and exercised in the army. It’s also because they don’t have a correct idea about the army and joining the army.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. That is the end of the test.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//3176ea24-f2d6-4c8b-ae31-83f2c4466dce/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_300_181_853_501.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A08%3A00Z%2F-1%2F%2F59f091d18fb01a4508dde513b1b3ab1de3684f3feab8d5b93d495bf4b76d6168" alt="Image" width="46%" /></div>


# 口试真题与提示（二）

# Part A (2 minutes)

Interlocutor:

– Good morning/afternoon. Could I have your mark sheets, please? Thank you.

(Hand over the mark sheets to the Assessor.)

- My name is... and this is my colleague... He / she is just going to be listening to us. So, you are Candidate A and Candidate B? Thank you.

- First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves. (Select one or more questions from each of the following categories as appropriate.)

(To Candidate A)

- Where do you live, the city or the country?

I live in the city now.

- Which do you think is better for living?

I think the country is better for living.

- Why?

Because the air there is cleaner and there is not so much traffic congestion.

– What's the problem of living in the city?

The biggest problem is traffic and air pollution.

Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, we'd like to know something about you, Candidate B.

(To Candidate B)

– What do you think of terrorism?

I think it is very frightening and inhumane.

- Do you think it is only a problem confined to one country?

No, I think it is a worldwide problem.

– When you heard the 911 Incident in America, how did you feel?

I felt very sad and indignant.

### 口试真题与提示

## 151

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//19abbef9-c9b8-447d-9403-e46ffafe90f9/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_300_180_854_501.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A08%3A01Z%2F-1%2F%2F07994e062dba21d9cf27b0ddad11fd134cf07234d3823ef2e3b43e7956327547" alt="Image" width="46%" /></div>


# 口试真题与提示（二）

# Part A (2 minutes)

Interlocutor:

- Good morning/afternoon. Could I have your mark sheets, please? Thank you.

(Hand over the mark sheets to the Assessor.)

- My name is... and this is my colleague... He/she is just going to be listening to us. So, you are Candidate A and Candidate B? Thank you.

- First of all, we'd like to know something about you, so I'm going to ask some questions about yourselves. (Select one or more questions from each of the following categories as appropriate.)

(To Candidate A)

- Where do you live, the city or the country?

I live in the city now.

- Which do you think is better for living?

I think the country is better for living.

- Why?

Because the air there is cleaner and there is not so much traffic congestion.

– What's the problem of living in the city?

The biggest problem is traffic and air pollution.

Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, we'd like to know something about you, Candidate B.

(To Candidate B)

– What do you think of terrorism?

I think it is very frightening and inhumane.

- Do you think it is only a problem confined to one country?

No, I think it is a worldwide problem.

– When you heard the 911 Incident in America, how did you feel?

I felt very sad and indignant.

– What do you think we should do?

I think we should work together to crack down terrorism.

#### Part B (3 minutes)

##### Interlocutor:

- Now I'd like you to talk about something between yourselves but speak loudly so that we can hear you. You should take care to share the opportunity of speaking.

(Put the pictures in front of both candidates and give instructions with reference to the pictures.)

- You may often do shopping. What will you do in such conditions? Discuss the picture and give your answers and reasons.

– This picture is for your reference. You have three minutes for this. Would you like to begin now, please?

##### Candidates:

A: We can see in the picture that the seller is trying to persuade the girl to buy the so-called cheap shirt. But is it really cheap? I think I won't buy this kind of clothes, because I think they are of poor quality.

B: I don't agree with you. Though the quality of them is not so good, they are very cheap and some are very fashionable. It can make one seem more individual.

A: Yes, they are cheap. But just as the saying goes "A bargain cannot be a good thing." And they are always on sale. After you take the last shirt, they will take out another last. This is just their trick of selling things.

B: Not always. Sometimes you can buy something of good quality in low price. But you have to take time to choose.

A: I think it's not worth the time. And most of the time we don't have the chance. We just waste our time in listening to their tricks.

B: But sometimes it can be fun.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you.

Picture

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//401dc5f0-a518-47f4-a5ad-07f7058b57ec/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_328_1185_913_1523.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-10-29T06%3A08%3A29Z%2F-1%2F%2Ffe0eb193dd21c0efbbb55a641841f01105ad093e2ad24d7ad4b2e666bc63103e" alt="Image" width="49%" /></div>


#### Part C (7 minutes)

##### Interlocutor:

- I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture.

(Put Picture 1 for Candidates in front of both candidates.)

- Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

- Candidate B, listen carefully while Candidate A is speaking. When he/she has finished, I'd like you to ask him/her a question about what he/she has said.

Candidate A, would you like to begin now, please?

Candidate A: (3 minutes)

This picture shows us a worrying but realistic situation: the official put the “public interests” above his head to show that he is working for the people, but below that he hold the “private interests” to his bosom, which tells us that this is what he really wants.

The picture tells us a very serious fact nowadays. Many officials try to seek personal gains under the guise of working for “the public interests”. Obviously they are very loyal to the people, but they just want to gain fame and personal interests at the same time. They deceive the people in this way to let them regard him as a good official. But what they really work for is their own interests. In doing so, they not only fool the people, but also damage the government’s image. So I think we need to take more serious measures to tackle with this problem.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, Candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

(Half a minute for asking and answering the question.)

Candidate B: What measures do you think we should take?

Candidate A: I think we should intensify supervision, and then let them have no chance to do so.

(Take back Picture 1 and put Picture 2 for Candidates in front of both candidates.)

- OK, Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

- Candidate A, listen carefully while Candidate B is speaking. When he/she has finished, I'd like you to ask him/her a question about what he/she has said.

- Candidate B, would you like to begin now, please?

Candidate B: (3 minutes)

As can be seen in the picture, there is a large warning on the wall — “Please put on safety helmet”. But the worker happily throws away the safety helmet just as if he didn’t see the sign. But what fall on his head will be accident.

The picture tells us a true situation. Although the attention to safe production by the government and the factory, some people still ignore this. They cherish an idea that accident won't happen to them. So in spite of the large sign and much attention to this, they still don't look on to it seriously. This is the main cause of many accidents. Their awareness of safe production is very dim. So even if they know the regulations, they still act on their own will, putting the rules aside. And then result would be very serious, accident will fall on their head, which is too late to regret. This kind of attitude not only harms themselves, but also bring great loss to the property of the country and the people. So we should try our best to eliminate this phenomenon.

### 口试真题与提示

#### Part C (7 minutes)

##### Interlocutor:

- I'm going to give each of you a picture and I'd like you to first briefly describe and then give your comment on what you see in the picture.

(Put Picture 1 for Candidates in front of both candidates.)

- Candidate A, this is your picture. You have three minutes to talk about it.

- Candidate B, listen carefully while Candidate A is speaking. When he/she has finished, I'd like you to ask him/her a question about what he/she has said.

Candidate A, would you like to begin now, please?

Candidate A: (3 minutes)

This picture shows us a worrying but realistic situation: the official put the “public interests” above his head to show that he is working for the people, but below that he hold the “private interests” to his bosom, which tells us that this is what he really wants.

The picture tells us a very serious fact nowadays. Many officials try to seek personal gains under the guise of working for “the public interests”. Obviously they are very loyal to the people, but they just want to gain fame and personal interests at the same time. They deceive the people in this way to let them regard him as a good official. But what they really work for is their own interests. In doing so, they not only fool the people, but also damage the government's image. So I think we need to take more serious measures to tackle with this problem.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, Candidate B, could you please ask your partner a question?

(Half a minute for asking and answering the question.)

Candidate B: What measures do you think we should take?

Candidate A: I think we should intensify supervision, and then let them have no chance to do so.

(Take back Picture 1 and put Picture 2 for Candidates in front of both candidates.)

- OK, Candidate B, here is your picture. You also have three minutes to talk about your picture.

- Candidate A, listen carefully while Candidate B is speaking. When he/she has finished, I'd like you to ask him/her a question about what he/she has said.

- Candidate B, would you like to begin now, please?

Candidate B: (3 minutes)

As can be seen in the picture, there is a large warning on the wall — “Please put on safety helmet”. But the worker happily throws away the safety helmet just as if he didn't see the sign. But what fall on his head will be accident.

The picture tells us a true situation. Although the attention to safe production by the government and the factory, some people still ignore this. They cherish an idea that accident won't happen to them. So in spite of the large sign and much attention to this, they still don't look on to it seriously. This is the main cause of many accidents. Their awareness of safe production is very dim. So even if they know the regulations, they still act on their own will, putting the rules aside. And then result would be very serious, accident will fall on their head, which is too late to regret. This kind of attitude not only harms themselves, but also bring great loss to the property of the country and the people. So we should try our best to eliminate this phenomenon.

##### Interlocutor:

- Thank you. Now, Candidate A, could you please ask your partner a question?

(Half a minute for asking and answering the question.)

Candidate A: How do you think can we eliminate this phenomenon?

Candidate B: First, I think we should intensify the propagation of safe production, and strengthen the education among workers and officials to make them develop the awareness of safe production. Second, we should intensify supervision and punishment to let them not dare to violate rules.

Interlocutor:

- Thank you. That is the end of the test.
